營收走勢圖

研究員/蔡佾璋 EASONTSAI@uni-psg.com

研究部維持南亞(1303)之投資建議為買進，主要考量：(1)南亞 7
月自結稅後 EPS 0.4 元，雖不如 6 月自結稅後 EPS 2.88 元，但因
6 月有包含權益法認列收益不具可比性，而具可比性的 4、5、7 月
自結稅前淨利率分別是 9.4%、13%、15.7%，歸母淨利率為 4.7%、
9.2%、10.4%，獲利顯著持續提升，符合研究部預期。營收方面，
7+8 月營收達成率 70.6%，優於預期，公司展望 9 月營收將優於 8
月，4Q26 的營收將優於 3Q26，整體營運維持正向。(2)應用於 AI
伺服器的 M6-M8 高階大訂單目前正與客戶洽談生產配合及廠區產
線對接等細節，預計將於 4Q26 完成協商並開始有明顯進展，帶動
後續營收與利潤大幅優化，去瓶頸後的 30 萬張 CCL 產能將全數投
入高階產品生產。(3)廠商持續將擴產資源湧入中高階材料，導致
以 PCB 為主軸的玻纖布、銅箔、CCL、ABF 載板等中低階電子材料
依然緊缺，研究部預期南亞電子材料漲價態勢將延續。此外，南亞
積極推動 AI 供應鏈高階材料的開發、認證與銷售，有顯著成效，
2027 年電子相關獲利占比也將提升至 98%，電子材料占比可望達
70-80%，優於研究部原先預估的65-70%。(4)子公司方面，受惠AI
需求旺盛 ， 南電產能有限但需求持續放大 ，ABF 載板漲價效應延續 ，
2026、2027 年業績皆會有明顯提升；南亞科受惠記憶體供需缺口
再擴大，利潤急速成長。(5)近期石化產品 EG 價格大漲，利差顯著
擴大，對獲利明顯幫助，若市況持續好轉，更明顯的獲利貢獻會落
在 4Q26。研究部分別上修南亞 2026 年、2027 年稅後 EPS 至 13.82
元、21.64 元(原估 12.02 元、16.94 元)，分別+2,325%YoY、
+56.6%YoY，年底淨值 69.8 元、91.4 元。研究部認為 2027 年 CSP
業者對整機成本及零組件價格控管趨嚴 ， 加上上游原料新增產能於
2H27 至1H28 陸續開出，電子材料及CCL 業者議價能力會降低，因
此將電子材料目標評價調降至 20 倍，惟考量目前股價隱含電子材
料評價僅約 12 倍，評價極低，故維持南亞買進投資建議。以2027
年電子材料:南亞科:傳產獲利占比 48:50:2，電子與傳產淨值占比
約 4：6 之比例推算，目標價調升至320 元(20xPER*2027 本業電子
材料 EPS + 5xPER*2027 南亞科貢獻稅後 EPS + 1xPBR*2027 傳產
BVPS)。

重點摘要： 
1. 南亞產品線包括在石化、塑膠、纖維與電子類，分佈廣泛

圖 1、南亞 1H26 部門別營收比重 
電子材料
52.7%
化工產品
16.9%
聚酯產品
15.8%
塑膠加工
12.7%
其他
1.9%

資料來源：公司、統一投顧整理

南亞是全球最大塑膠加工廠，此外，其電子級玻纖絲為全球
第二大 ， 銅箔基板 、 玻纖布為全球第三大 ， 乙二醇EG為全球第
四大，環氧樹脂、丙二酚BPA為全球第五大業者。南亞產能全
球布局，主要生產基地在台灣，有57個廠， 中國有34個， 美國
有8個廠，共99個生產廠。

同時，南亞持有 26.3%的南亞科為全球第四大 DRAM廠，持有
61.4%的子公司南電為 「PCB三雄」 之一 ， 穩居全球前五大IC載
板廠，也是全球前十大PCB製造商。南亞的產品線為台塑集團
中最廣泛，分佈在石化、 纖維 、塑膠加工及電子材料等，1H26
電子材料佔比由 2025年的46.4% 提升到52.7%，化工產品由
21.8%降至16.9%，聚酯產品由16%略降至15.8%，塑膠產品由
13.9%降至12.7%， 其他1.9%。 公司生產的原材料多為集團內部
自用 ， 僅約少部分對外銷售 ， 外售若對公司更有幫助的話 ， 公
司將會提高外售比率。

聚酯產品包括聚酯薄膜、聚酯纖維(如聚酯棉、POY、DTY以及
瓶用酯粒)等 。 電子材料產品包括銅箔 、 銅箔基板(CCL)、 玻纖
絲、玻纖布、環氧樹脂(EPOXY)等；塑膠產品包括膠布、膠皮
與PU合成皮等。銷售地區廣泛，以中國、美國、台灣為主。

2. 子公司南電、南亞科漲價趨勢延續，母公司南亞間接受惠 
由於AI應用需求大增 ，ASIC需求預估在2026年大幅增加 ， 此類
產品全都用ABF載板 ，ABF載板於4Q25起漲 ，1Q26及2Q26皆維持
續漲價節奏。研究部認為接下來ABF廠議價能力轉強，南電報
價得以持續上漲，母公司南亞間接受惠。

南亞科2Q26持續繳出亮眼的成績，GM及OPM分別達到79.5%及
73.7%的水準 ， 不亞於一線大廠 ， 單季稅後EPS 14.66元 。 南亞
科預期3Q26營運及OPM將進一步提升 ， 且短缺情況至4Q26未見
變化 ， 同時預期市場在2028年前不會發生反轉 ， 母公司南亞間
接受惠。

股權變動方面，董事會之前決議處分南電 3%與南亞科1%的股
權 ， 其中 ， 南電的第一個3%已經處分完畢 ， 後來董事會又通過
再處分3%， 大部分已於2Q26底處分完畢 ， 目前沒有新的處分計
畫，兩次重大處分後仍持有南電61%股權。

### Page 4

請參見末頁重要批露及免責聲明  4

2026/3/25南亞科董事會宣布晟碟 （Sandisk） 、 鎧俠 （Kioxia）、
SK Hynix旗下的Solidigm、思科（Cisco）四家公司分三次私
募認購普通股共3.5億股 ， 股權稀釋約11.3%,研究部推算目前
南亞持有南亞科之持股比例約為26.3%。 而南亞持有南亞科的
3,099萬股權處分目前尚未執行，年底前也不一定會處分，預
估執行後持股比例將調整至25.4%。

3. 電子材料營收自1H26的52.7%提升至9月的60%以上

表1、電子材料為南亞獲利主體(營業利益佔比) 
資料來源：公司、統一投顧整理

缺貨造成電子材料持續漲價，2025年初以來最先出現明顯供
給吃緊的玻纖 ， 近期已擴散至銅箔 、 銅箔基板等材料 ， 各材料
缺貨、漲價還會延續一段時間。中低階產品缺貨主因在於高
階化帶來的產能排擠，隨著玻纖、銅箔及基板高階產品製程
精度提升，生產速度下降，不論銅箔、玻纖、基板，要生產1
單位的高階材料，都必須放棄生產很多單位的中低階材料，
導致缺貨更嚴重，此情況將維持未來一段時間。

電子材料持續成為南亞營運轉型核心，產線維持高稼動率，
銅箔基板、玻纖布與玻纖絲產能利用率達滿載，銅箔與環氧
樹脂產能利用率在8-9成之間。8月電子材料營收占比已提升
至59.8%，較7月增加2.8pct， 正式逼近六成 。 電子材料產業到
2028、2029年仍可維持榮景。

業界有開始擴產，但擴產速度本身有限制，且擴產後也還是
會面臨材料的問題，此外，PCB層數大幅增加，將使得供給增
加，幅度仍跟不上需求的增速 。 整體而言 ， 電子材料供需偏緊
與漲價趨勢短期難以扭轉 ， 銅箔基板及高階PCB的浪潮會有望
延續。

### Page 5

請參見末頁重要批露及免責聲明  5

4. 玻纖獲利率最高，獲利貢獻CCL最大，全品項OPM持續提升 
表 2、南亞各產品別 OPM 逐季轉佳 
資料來源：公司、統一投顧整理

南亞產品價格漲幅因客戶依賴度而異，依客戶每個月機動調
整 。 由於中高階產品供不應求 ， 加上同業將產能轉做高階 ， 導
致一般消費性電子用的E-Glass產能吃緊 ， 這對產品報價相當
有利，報價自2025年開始每個月約調漲5-10%。

獲利率方面，現階段電子材料獲利率以玻纖紗/布排行第一、
近期CCL跟載板漲幅度大，獲利率已逐漸接近玻纖，排名升至
第二，再來是銅箔，而環氧樹脂有8成為非電子用途，因此獲
利率較銅箔低些。

獲利金額貢獻方面，雖然目前獲利率最高的是玻纖紗/布，但
CCL營收規模較大，所以獲利貢獻金額超過玻纖。

持續漲價對3Q26、4Q26利潤率是很重要因素，但除了漲價之
外 ， 產能利用率拉高 ， 固定成本不變 ， 因此訂單及出貨量增加
對利潤率也有一定貢獻， 產品組合改變也很重要， 整體來說 ，
電子材料全產品項的OPM皆持續提升當中。

電子材料產品主要包括： 
I.玻纖布 
南亞的玻纖布產能排名為全球第三，佔南亞的營收提升至 6-
7%，但獲利貢獻位居前列。年產能約5.9億米(以一般布計)，
自用比重原為40%-50%， 但為優先確保下游高階CCL供料無虞 ，
外銷比例已調降至30%-40%(自用仍以市價計價，不影響實質
利潤)，稼動率自6月起持續滿載。受全球嚴重缺料帶動，7-9
月玻纖布價格強勁上揚，預期4Q26動能不減。玻纖布/紗獲利
率為電子材料中最高，2Q26 OPM已達30%-40%以上。

### Page 6

請參見末頁重要批露及免責聲明  6

日系知名同業大廠為玻纖紗 /布領導廠商，將專注於高階市
場，而Low DK1玻纖布市場供不應求 ， 進而尋找南亞進行合作 。
此日本同業大廠提供玻纖紗，而南亞可提供Low DK1產能，製
成玻纖布後再回送日廠做後段處理。

此外，日本同業大廠也有供應Low DK2玻纖紗給南亞，織成布
後製成自家CCL基板可對外銷售，Low DK2 產品預計2026年內
會量產 ， 屬長期技術互惠合作 。 初期量不大 ， 但3Q26起日東紡
供貨量將明顯增加，足以支撐公司M8以上高階基板需求，對
於公司後續CCL發展，以及高階玻纖數量擴大會有一定幫助。

E-Glass部分 ， 南亞表示各類電子與消費型載板 、PCB層數持續
升級，即使終端消費總量持平，對E-Glass的用料需求依然持
續擴大。E-Glass自2025年底起漲勢確立，3Q26價格仍持續上
漲，公司在9月會議中表示，中國同業於年中報價約2.2美元/
米，4Q26期貨已漲至2.5美元/米 ， 公司預期2027年有機會上看
3美元/米，漲價動能依然非常強勁。南亞E-Glass採大窯爐生
產，與特殊玻纖的坩堝製程不衝突。

同業因原料緊缺或利潤考量放棄中低階訂單，客戶大量轉向
原料掌握度極高的南亞，使南亞在中階與消費型基板 (如M4
等)的稼動率維持滿載 ， 南亞不會放棄E-Glass供應 ， 未來將憑
藉原料自給優勢，承接大量同業外溢轉單，充分享受價量齊
揚帶來的超額利潤。

特殊玻纖紗使用白金坩堝設備，包含坩堝設計、冷卻拉絲等
環節有許多細微變化，會影響產品的介電常數。全球高階豐
田織布機排單已至2028-2029年 ， 南亞因為有超前部署織布機
產能 ， 提早下訂排單 ， 自4Q26起每年均有新機台如期到位加入
生產。透過台灣新港與昆山廠增設坩堝擴產，2026-2028年兩
岸累計將新增達100多個紡位。

高階石英布（Q-Glass）南亞在技術上仍需突破，初期石英紗
會先從外部供應商取得 ， 但因為公司具備生產玻纖紗的經驗 ，
目前還沒放棄，持續積極投入自主研發。

### Page 7

請參見末頁重要批露及免責聲明  7

II.銅箔 
南亞的銅箔產能排名全球第二，近期銅箔佔南亞營收已超過
10%。銅箔年產能約為11萬多噸，目前產能利用率滿載，自用
比例約50%。公司計畫在惠州擴建銅箔產線，將增加2.34萬噸
產能 ， 約擴張2成 ， 預計於2028年1月投產 。 台灣彰濱地區也有
銅箔擴產規劃，主要針對PCB應用為主。

南亞的銅箔目前銷量以HVLP1/2為主 ，HVLP3穩定量產 ， 且具備
HVLP 3.5(介於3與4之間的自有規格)及小量HVLP 4生產能力 ，
另外也有一些特定規格之超薄銅箔已進入量產，HVLP 5 則是
持續開發當中，產品線正積極朝向高階發展。

AI伺服器對超低損耗要求嚴格，高階銅箔結晶晶粒需極細，
製程具物理極限使生產速度大幅變慢，投下相同資源，高階
產出噸數遠低於傳統規格，產生嚴重產能排擠效應。

加工費方面，過去多逐季協商、具價格僵固性， 但自7、8月起
開始出現密集且連續調漲 。3Q26大宗銅箔每噸售價約1.7-1.8
萬美元(含銅價約1.4萬)，加工費約3-4千美元/噸。因結構性
生產降速與高階需求不可逆，預期加工費將具備永久性上升
動能，後續極有機會突破4-5千美元/噸。

III.銅箔基板(CCL) 
南亞的銅箔基板 （CCL） 產能排名全球第三 ， 目前CCL兩岸產能
約618萬張/月，台灣 ：中國崑山與惠州約3：7， 目前稼動率滿
載，公司表示1H26 M4 以上基板銷量逾200萬張。台灣樹林與
新港廠區既有設備的小幅改造去瓶頸進度超前，已提前於
4Q26初實現30萬張產能提升 ， 約+5%YoY， 將全數投入M7以上高
階訂單。

CCL佔南亞的營收比重已於 7月達30%左右，M系列佔其中的
2/3(約整體營收20%)，M4佔M系列的營收60%(約CCL營收40%，
約整體營收12%)，其他規格(M6至M8)則是整體營收8%。

### Page 8

請參見末頁重要批露及免責聲明  8

南亞CCL具備M4至M9等級量產能力，其中，M4是出貨量最大產
品，主要供應NVIDIA用於RTX 50 系列顯卡。1H26 M6-M8處於
客戶開發與送樣階段 ， 與多位知名終端客戶與CSP客戶積極洽
談大單，預計4Q26協商將陸續完成，並將迎來明顯放量(合約
會要求客戶提供保障資金)，產品組合升級亦帶動營收、獲利
明顯提升 。M9目前則是已具量產能力並進入客戶認證測試中 ，
數據回饋理想且有極小量試賣，預計大批量產時程將落在  
1H27。

CCL自4Q25起CCL每月維持3-5%漲幅，7、8月部分客戶補漲達
10-15%，4Q26將延續逐月調漲步調 。 南亞不放棄利潤依然可觀
且客戶轉單持續湧入的M4，採 「高中低階全線掌握」 ，藉由產
品組合優化大幅推升CCL獲利率 （2Q26 OPM近20%，3Q、4Q26將
進一步追趕目前玻纖的40% OPM水準）。

儘管目前台光電在M9市佔率最高，然南亞的策略是透過開發
M10等高階產品來實現「彎道超車」，一舉超越競爭對手。南
亞的M10採用自製配方，即K2特殊玻纖布搭配HVP4銅箔，以及
自研的碳氫樹脂，不需仰賴昂貴且進口申請繁瑣的石英布方
案，在未來的成本控制與產能擴張上，將具備極佳的競爭優
勢。M10目前則是還在認證中，實測Dk/Df數據已達M10理想水
準且優於同業 ， 因測試需時1-2千小時 。 整體認證持續推進中 ，
主控權在客戶端 ， 希望1H27取得更具體的驗證成果 ， 若認證順
利通過的話，2H27將逐漸放量。

5. 預估3Q26稅後EPS 4.28元，+942.7%YoY 
受惠於AI發展帶動中高階電子材料供應緊缺、價量齊揚，且
中東戰事帶動化工、聚酯、塑膠加工等產品售價隨原料行情
飆升 ， 南亞2Q26營收合計836.5億元 ，+21.9%QoQ，+27.3%YoY，
營收達成率97%， 符合預期 。 營業利益113.6億元 ，+203.5%QoQ，
+772%YoY， 主要受惠於AI產業擴大投資 ， 相關材料需求持續成
長，使電路板等電子材料產品利潤提高。而權益法投資(台塑
化、南亞科、台朔重工、麥寮汽電、台塑烯烴(美國))共認列
獲利190.9億元(原估145億元)， 優於預期 。2Q26歸屬母公司淨
利267.5億元(原估183.2億元)， 獲利達成率146%， 優於預期 ，
稅後EPS 3.37元(原估2.31元)，+87.7%QoQ，YoY虧轉盈。

展望2H26，雖預期3Q26聚酯部門因紡織用途市況不佳 ， 營收看
季減 ， 化工部門營收看持平 ， 然而 ， 塑膠加工部門營收穩健季
增，且公司看好AI算力規模於未來幾年內仍會大幅成長，邊
緣運算將帶動終端電子材料全系列產品升級，高階材料銷售
比重逐漸提高，電子材料將帶動南亞整體營收將持續向上攀
升，預期9月營運表現將優於8月，3Q26優於2Q26，4Q26優於
3Q26。。研究部預估3Q26營收924.3億元(原估877.9億元)，
+10.5%QoQ ，+44%YoY ， 營 業 利 益144.3 億元， +27.1%QoQ ，
+1,293%YoY ，稅後淨利 339.5 億元 ( 原估 268.4 億元 ) ，
+26.9%QoQ，+943%YoY，稅後EPS 4.28元(原估3.38元)。

6. 預估 2026 、2027 年稅後 EPS 13.82 元、 21.64 元 ， 分 別
+2,325%YoY、+56.6%YoY  
展望未來，研究部認為南亞電子材料事業持續增溫，公司整
體營運將進一步轉佳。預估 2026年營收3,416.8億元(原估
3,287.9億元)，+31.5%YoY，毛利率20.4%，+12.1pctYoY。全
年營業利益478.3億元，+1,192%YoY，本業營運明顯回升，加
上權益法認列轉投資子公司獲利大增 ， 預估2026全年稅後EPS 
13.82元(原估12.02元)，+2,325%YoY。

預估2027年營收4,533億元(原估3,788億元)，+32.7%YoY，毛
利率30.9%，+10.5pctYoY。全年營業利益1,099.5億元(原估
922億元)，+129.8%YoY， 本業營運持續提升 ， 權益法認列轉投
資子公司獲利進一步擴大 ， 預估2027全年稅後淨利1,717億元
(原估1,344億元)，+56.6%YoY，EPS 21.64元(原估16.94元)。

數以上成長。

季持續增加，美系記憶體及 IDM 客戶，中砂將持續增加市占率
及逐季放量。

高階產品組合比重將持續提高，以提升營收及毛利率。

2 群益投顧 
  ESG ：  
E：(Environmental) 。中砂秉持企業經營須兼顧環境保護之理念，持續落實環境
友善管理策略與措施。國際永續趨勢與國家 2050 淨零路徑推動下，中砂透過
執行組織型溫室氣體盤查、產品碳足跡盤查及導入循環經濟專案，循序漸進達
成減碳目標。  
此外，中砂重視氣候變遷伴隨之風險與機會，亦將氣候行動納入公司風險管理
與營運策略，積極研擬因應策略及應變措施；對於水資源、空氣污染、廢棄物
等環境議題，更推行各項管理與減量方案，朝向永續經營之願景邁進。  
S： (Social) 。中砂長期深耕地方社區創生與投入藝文教育與活動，使參與社會
公益成為中砂的企業文化。秉持著「共好：你好、我好、大家好」的經營理念
及懷著「知恩、感恩、報恩」的心，中砂持續與當地縣市政府、學校、社福機
構團體與自身基金會合作，攜手保留珍貴產業文化，提倡書法教育，為當地社
區帶來實質性的協助與回饋，發揮企業正向影響力。  
中砂座落於台灣著名的陶瓷之鄉 — 鶯歌，期望與鄉里共榮，為社區民眾打造友
善的生活環境，因此長期參與社區關懷與愛鄉感恩公益，連續多年贊助新北市
鶯歌區西鶯里、中湖里的敬老活動與銀髮族共餐活動，並致贈年節禮盒及物資
予鄰里長輩。近年中砂更與民間公益培訓單位合作，透過「學生提案，企業協
力」的方式，與公益團體合作推出聯名禮盒、愛心送餐等創新專案。除了達到
支援公益團體的目的外，中砂更將聯名禮盒分送給鶯歌的鄰里長輩，持續傳遞
公益善心。  
G： (Governance) 。企業永續發展的關鍵建基在健全且完善的公司治理架構上，
使得強化董事會監督管理職能成為中砂精進公 司治理的核心目標。除了保障股
東權益外，中砂更積極建構具備應對傳統及氣候變遷、資訊安全等新興風險的
管理機制，透過完整的鑑別、評估及擬定因應措施的流程，降低潛在風險對公
司營運之衝擊。在持續提升營運韌性的同時，也為利害關係人創造更大的價值。
公司簡介：  
中國砂輪股份有限公司成立於 07/1964 ，總部位於鶯歌，主要從事生產及銷售
砂輪、鑽石碟、再生晶圓等研磨工具。  
公司早期產品以應用於石材加工的傳統砂輪為主，後因取得鑽石碟技術的專利
授權及台系大客戶供應鏈在地化策略，以鑽石碟產品切入半導體產業領域。
2020 年，公司再生晶圓事業部竹科廠於竹南科 學園區落成啟用量產，成為全球
前三大再生晶圓供應商。  
再生晶圓主要台灣同業包含辛耘 (3583 TT) 、昇陽半導體 (8028 TT) ；鑽石碟為
3M(MMM US) 、Abrasive Technology 、Asahi(4216 JP) 、Seasol( 韓 )等。中砂大客
戶包含台系晶圓代工廠、美系記憶體廠等。  
中砂自 04/2025 開始認列日本三井金屬礦業集團旗下的兩家公司 MKS 與 MGT，
歸類在傳統砂輪事業部 (ABU) 。  
營收占比：  
傳統砂輪事業部 (ABU) ，毛利率 20~25% 。用於工具機、機械，近期還有包含半
導體 (碳化矽 )的產業。

ABU ：受惠於傳產機械與工具機砂輪需求回溫。 DBU ：受惠於 CMP 鑽石碟於

貨。 SBU ： 12 吋月產能 36 萬片滿載。  
2026 年 CAPEX 與擴產計畫：  
中砂 2026 年 CAPEX 約 15 億元， 4Q26 鑽石碟月產能預計增加至 7.0 萬顆、
4Q27 增加至 9.5 萬顆； 4Q26 12 吋再生晶圓月產能將增加至 40 萬片， 8 吋約
15 萬片，共 55 萬片。  
資料來源：中砂法說簡報。  
中砂 2026 年各事業群展望：  
傳統砂輪事業部 (ABU) ： 2026 年將受惠需求回溫及新砂輪子公司合併綜效，目
標營收較 2025 雙位數以上成長。  
鑽石碟事業部 (DBU) ：先進製程及先進封裝之鑽石碟需求將逐季持續增加，美
系記憶體及 IDM 客戶，中砂將持續增加市占率及逐季放量。  
晶圓事業部 (SBU) ： 2027 年將增加至 12 吋的月產能到 43 萬片，高階產品組合
比重將持續提高，以提升營收及毛利率。

7 群益投顧 
  【投資評等說明】  
  評等  定義  
  強力買進 (Strong Buy)  首次評等潛在上漲空間 ≥ 35%  
  買進 (Buy)  15% ≤首次評等潛在上漲空間 <35%  
  區間操作 (Trading Buy) 5% ≤首次評等潛在上漲空間 <15%

中立 (Neutral)  
無法由基本面給予投資評等  
預期近期股價將處於盤整  
建議降低持股

【投資評等調整機制】  
  投資評等係「首次」給予特定個股投資評等時，其前一日收盤價相較 3 個月
目標價之潛在上漲空間計算而得。個股投資評等分為四個等級，定義如上。
爾後的投資評等係依循「首次評等」，直到停止推薦。   
「強力買進」、「買進」及「區間操作」均有 upside 目標價。差別僅在於，
首次評等時潛在報酬率不同。「中立」則無目標價。  
一旦我們給予特定個股「強力買進」 、 「買進」或「區間操作」之投資評等，
就是責任的開始，爾後將透過各式研究報告作定期性、持續性追蹤基本面及
股價變化，直到停止推薦。  
停止推薦情境：  
1.  達目標價。  
2.  雖未達原訂目標價，但檢視基本面、訊息面、籌碼面等多方訊息，研
判股價上漲空間已然有限，將適時出具降評報告。  
3.  推薦後股價不漲反跌。

【免責聲明】  
  本研究報告僅提供予特定人之客戶作為參考資料「非經同意不得轉載」 。我
們並不確保此資訊的完整性與正確性，投資人應了解，報告中有關未來預測
之陳述可能不會實現，因而不應被依賴。而且此報告並非根據特定投資目的
或依預定對象之財務狀況所撰寫出來的，因此，此研究報告的目的，既非對
投資人於買賣證券、選擇權、期貨或其他證券相關之衍生性商品提供詢價服
務，亦非作為進行交易的要約。投資人應注意到相關證券之價值及收益，可
能會有無預警地上升或下降，產生投資回報金額可能比原始投資來得少的
情形。

營收走勢圖

玻璃陶瓷–電子材料  
富喬(1815.TT) 
AI 材料升級趨勢確立，高階玻纖布開啟成長新局

富喬為台灣少數具備紗、布垂直整合與自有配方能力的玻纖布廠。2H26 
受惠二代低介電布放量、報價調漲及良率改善，營收與 獲利可望逐季創
高；進入 2027 年後，獲利成長動能將由高階產品占比提升與新增產能
貢獻。台灣廠持續將既有產能轉向二代低介電布，東莞廠自 1Q27 起分
批將 E 布產能升級至二代布，泰國一期則自 2H27 起逐步投產，承接
客戶對非中國高階產能的需求，加上低膨脹布開始導入，產品組合將進
一步往高階化發展。研究部預估富喬 2026、2027 年稅後 EPS 分別為 
5.13 元與 9.30 元，稅後淨利分別年增 267.9% 與 81.4%。考量富喬
為日系龍頭以外少數具備垂直整合與自有配方能力的供應商，以  2027 
年獲利為評價基礎，給予 20 倍本益比，目標價 185 元，初次給予買進
投資評等。

1. 規格升級推動產品組合高階化 
AI 伺服器與高速交換器的傳輸速率持續提升 ， 板材由 M7、M8 向 M9 以
上升級，玻纖布同步由 E-glass 轉向一代、二代低介電布。石英布因鑽
孔與除膠渣良率受限而導入遞延，終端客戶轉回採用二代低介電布，延
長其產品週期。研究部預估富喬二代低介電布營收占比將由 2025 年的 
7%，提升至 2026 年的 27% 與 2027 年的 51%。

2. 既有機台重新配置，毛利率大幅跳升 
玻纖布織布機由豐田獨家供應 ， 新機交期已排至 2031 年，2027 年以前
產業新增供給主要仰賴既有機台重新配置 。 富喬現有約 920 台織機 ， 憑
藉紗、布垂直整合，可在無須大幅增加資本支出下將既有產能轉向二代
低介電布 ， 帶動毛利率由 2025 年的 27.6%， 升至 2026 年的 44.7% 與 
2027 年的 54.8%。

### Page 2

請參見末頁重要披露及免責聲明 2

公司簡介 
富喬工業（1815 TT）成立於 1999 年，為台灣少數具備玻璃纖維紗至玻璃纖
維布垂直整合能力的材料廠，主要產品涵蓋電子級、工業級玻纖紗及電子級玻
纖布，其中電子級玻纖布為銅箔基板（CCL）與印刷電路板（PCB）的關鍵上游
材料。公司目前主要生產基地位於雲林斗六、虎尾及中國東莞，並已布局泰國
新產能，以強化非中國供應鏈配置。相較單純織布廠，富喬具備自有玻纖紗生
產與配方研發能力，可依終端規格變化調整紗種與產品組合。近年受惠 AI 伺
服器、高速交換器等應用帶動 PCB 材料規格持續升級，富喬營運重心逐步由
傳統 E-glass 轉向低介電等高階電子級材料。

玻纖布產業介紹 
玻纖布位於 PCB 材料供應鏈最上游，製程橫跨玻璃熔煉與紡織兩個領域。玻
纖原料經窯爐熔融後 ， 由鉑銠漏板拉製成單絲 ， 再經上漿 、 集束與加撚形成電
子紗 ， 後續以織機織成玻纖布 ， 並經開纖及矽烷偶聯劑等後處理後 ， 交由 CCL 
廠含浸樹脂製成膠片，再與銅箔壓合成銅箔基板，最終應用於 PCB 或載板。 
玻纖布規格主要可由織物型號 、 紗徑與玻璃配方三個面向區分 。 以織物型號而
言，7628 屬厚布，基重約 210 g/m²，2116、1080 依序變薄，10 系列則屬超
薄布；若依紗徑區分，粗紗單纖直徑約 9 至 13 微米，細紗則降至約 4 至 
9 微米，隨紗徑變細，斷絲風險與製程難度同步提高，織機亦須降速，超薄布
生產效率僅約厚布的二分之一至三分之一 。 另一方面 ， 玻璃配方則直接決定材
料電性 ， 介電損耗亦隨材料世代持續下降 。 隨高速傳輸規格提升 ，玻纖與樹脂
之間的介電差異會放大訊號時延與織紋效應 ， 因此傳輸速率每提升一個世代 ，
材料亦須同步升級。 
除玻璃配方外，織布後的開纖與表面處理亦是高階玻纖布性能的關鍵。即使
CCL 廠商採用相同原料，不同玻纖布廠的產品在電性、耐熱性與良率上的表現
仍可能存在顯著差異，也使高階玻纖布認證週期通常長達兩年以上。就材料成
本結構而言，玻纖布占傳統 CCL 材料成本約 25% 至 40%，若升級至石英布，
占比可提高至約六成；隨高階 PCB 對 CCL 規格與用量提升，CCL 占 PCB 用
料成本比重亦由過去三至四成提升至五成以上。玻纖布因此已由過去的通用材
料，逐步轉變為影響高速板材性能、成本與供應穩定性的關鍵材料。

富喬為台灣少數具備玻纖紗、布
垂直整合與自有配方能力的材料
廠，近年受惠 AI 帶動高階 PCB 
材料升級 ， 營運重心正由傳統 E-
glass 轉向低介電玻纖布，並同
步布局泰國非中國產能。

玻纖布位於 PCB 材料供應鏈最
上游 ， 製程與認證門檻高 ， 且隨高
速傳輸推升材料規格升級，其成
本占比與對板材性能的重要性同
步提升，已由通用材料轉為高階  
PCB 的關鍵材料。

### Page 4

請參見末頁重要披露及免責聲明 4

AI 世代推升高速材料規格持續升級 
AI 基礎建設對材料的需求，並非僅來自伺服器出貨量成長，更重要的是運算
密度與傳輸速率持續提升，帶動 PCB 材料規格同步升級。相較傳統伺服器約
三至四年的平台週期，AI 伺服器迭代速度明顯加快，GPU、ASIC 與交換器間
的資料傳輸需求快速增加，推動網路架構由 400G、800G 向 1.6T、3.2T 演
進。隨訊號速率提升，傳輸損耗與訊號完整性要求同步提高，板材須持續降低
介電常數與介電損耗，帶動 CCL 由 M7、M8 向 M9 以上升級；玻纖布作為影
響板材電性的重要材料，亦由傳統 E-glass 逐步升級至一代、二代低介電
布，並進一步朝石英布與第三代低介電材料發展。 
就目前材料演進路徑而言，400G 交換器主要採用 M7 板材搭配一代低介電
布，800G 升級至 M8 後，一代與二代低介電布開始並用；進入 1.6T 世代
後，二代低介電布的重要性進一步提升，部分高階規格則開始評估導入石英
布。展望 2028 年後，隨 3.2T 逐步導入，材料規格預期將進一步朝升級版石
英布或第三代低介電布發展，高階玻纖布的滲透率持續提升。 
研究部認為，規格升級將成為本輪玻纖布產業成長的核心驅動力。隨 AI 運算
與高速傳輸規格持續演進，材料需求將由傳統 E-glass 加速轉向低介電、低
膨脹等高階產品。在終端平台持續更替的背景下，玻纖布須隨板材世代同步升
級，而每一次規格提升亦伴隨 ASP 與單位產值增加，使產業成長不僅來自需
求量擴張，更來自產品組合持續高階化。

表二、CCL 等級與對應玻纖布

升，帶動 PCB 材料由 E-glass 
向低介電、低膨脹等高階玻纖布
升級，推升高階產品滲透率、ASP 
與單位產值，成為產業成長的核
心驅動力。

### Page 5

請參見末頁重要披露及免責聲明 5

專利與認證構成高階玻纖布的實質進入障礙 
高階玻纖布的核心技術門檻在玻纖紗配方，低介電與低膨脹材料的關鍵專利亦
主要集中於紗端，後進者若要切入，須重新設計配方以迴避既有專利，良率與
量產時程因而受限。另一方面，CCL 廠需將特定玻纖布與樹脂、銅箔共同調
校，一旦納入合格供應商名單，更換材料通常須重新開立料號並進行完整驗
證，使認證週期可長達數年。富喬憑藉紗、布垂直整合，可直接由紗端調整配
方與製程，並已完成多地高階低介電玻纖布專利布局，低膨脹布亦已突破既有
專利限制並通過終端認證。研究部認為專利保護與長認證週期共同構成高階玻
纖布的實質進入障礙，亦使中國廠商產能雖快速擴張，短期內能實際轉化為高
階產品供給的比例仍有限。

供需失衡與料源綁定 
玻纖布供需失衡自 2026 年起由通用布延伸至高階布，主要瓶頸集中於織機、
良率與認證。玻纖布織機由豐田獨家供應，新機交期已排至 2031 年，且超薄
與特殊布須降速生產，使名目機台數無法等比轉化為實際產出；另一方面，高
階布認證週期普遍超過兩年，也使既有產能難以在短期內快速切換料號。 
E 布短缺主要來自龍頭廠商退出低階產能。日東紡、旭化成陸續停產 E-glass 
布後，供給收縮帶動各類 E 布報價自 2025 年底至 2026 年普遍上漲逾一倍
以上。惟中國新增產能自 2027 年陸續開出，加上消費性需求仍偏弱，研究部
預期 E 布供需將於 2027 年逐步緩解，報價漲幅趨緩。 
一代低介電布供需相對平衡，惟隨二代布需求放大，具備雙線能力的廠商傾向
將織機轉向二代布。二代低介電布則為目前供給最為吃緊的品項，主要對應 
M8 以上板材。除織機供給受限外，良率更是限制有效產出的關鍵，新進者量
產良率多不足五成，使名目產能與實際出貨量存在明顯落差，也支撐二代布報
價維持在一代布的約 2 至 3 倍。 
低膨脹布的供給缺口則可能延續更久。隨先進封裝尺寸放大與層數增加，而供
應仍高度集中於日東紡，新產能最快自 2H27 起陸續開出。石英布則面臨不同
瓶頸，問題不在供給，而在加工良率與導入進度；由於鑽孔與除膠渣難度較
高，部分原規劃採用石英布的設計轉回二代低介電布，反而延長二代布的產品
週期。 
供給吃緊亦同步改變材料認證與採購模式。過去玻纖布多由 CCL 廠與板廠選
料，現階段雲端業者與系統廠已逐步直接認證布廠並指定料源，部分客戶另要
求非中國產能，CCL 廠亦透過長約提前鎖定高階布供給。高階玻纖布的競爭因
而由單純產能擴張，轉向認證、良率與料源掌握能力的競爭。

高階玻纖布具備配方專利與長認
證週期雙重門檻，限制新進者有
效產能釋放，也使富喬憑藉紗布
垂直整合與既有認證基礎建立明
顯競爭優勢。

玻纖布供給受限於織機、良率與
認證門檻，高階產品短缺延續並
推動客戶直接綁定料源，產業競
爭 重 心 由 產 能 擴 張 轉 向 認 證 能
力、量產良率與供應穩定性。

### Page 6

請參見末頁重要披露及免責聲明 6

圖三、二代低介電布供需缺口

資料來源：統一投顧預估

產能配置與產品組合 
富喬目前共有約 920 台織布機，其中台灣 490 台、東莞 430 台。受限於織
布機交期，富喬 2027 年以前的主要成長動能，是將既有機台由 E 布、一代
低介電布持續轉向單位產值與毛利率更高的二代低介電布，透過產品組合升級
放大既有產能效益。 
台灣廠部分機台維持生產 E 布，供應台系 CCL 廠與低軌衛星應用，其餘產能
則持續轉向二代低介電布，低膨脹布亦預計自 2027 年起逐步導入。海外方
面，東莞現有 430 台於 2026 年仍以 E 布為主，自 1Q27 起採台灣供紗、當
地織布模式分批轉作二代低介電布。泰國一期則規劃新增 500 台織機，預計
自 2H27 起分批到位，主要生產低介電布並承接客戶對非中國產能的需求。 
富喬能快速進行跨廠區與跨產品配置，關鍵在於紗、布垂直整合。公司自有電
子級玻纖紗可隨布種升級同步調整紗的配方與規格，降低對外部紗源的依賴。
隨台灣產能持續高階化、東莞轉產二代布及泰國新產能加入，研究部預估二代
低介電布營收占比將由 2025 年的 7%，提升至 2026 年的 27% 與 2027 年的 
51%，同期毛利率亦由 27.6% 升至 44.7% 與 54.8%。

富喬以既有機台轉產高毛利二代
低介電布，並透過東莞轉線與泰
國新產能擴充高階產品供給，帶
動二代布營收占比與整體毛利率
持續提升。

圖四、富喬產品組合演變（營收占比）

營運概況 
1. 2H26 營運將改寫新高 
富喬 7、8 月營收分別為 8.54 億元（+24.0% MoM、+68.1% YoY）與 9.12 億
元（+6.8% MoM、+79.2% YoY） ，連續改寫單月新高。隨新一代 AI 伺服器與 
1.6T 交換器平台陸續放量，二代低介電布需求同步攀升，加以 6 月起調漲的
報價自 3Q26 全季認列，研究部預估 3Q26 營收 27.66 億元（+29.8% QoQ、
+80.4% YoY） ，改寫單季歷史新高。毛利率升至 48.8%（+8.8ppt QoQ、
+21.9ppt YoY） ，除產品組合與報價效益外，二代布量產良率亦已逐步改善。
營業利益 10.39 億元（+89.8% QoQ、+301.5% YoY） ，營益率 37.6%；稅後淨
利 8.58 億元（+1.8% QoQ、+203.2% YoY），EPS 1.40 元，EPS 較 2Q26 小幅
下滑係因盈餘轉增資後股數增加。展望 4Q26，隨著二代布出貨比重持續提升
以及良率續揚貢獻，研究部預估營收 30.51 億元（+10.3% QoQ、+86.4% 
YoY） 、毛利率 49.9%、EPS 1.62 元。

2. 2027 年成長動能轉向產品組合升級，EPS 估 9.30 元 
2027 年富喬獲利成長動能將由價格上漲轉向產品組合升級與新增產能貢獻。
台灣廠持續將既有機台由一代轉向二代低介電布，東莞廠自 1Q27 起採台灣供
紗、當地織布模式，分批將 E 布產能轉作二代布；泰國一期則自 2H27 起逐
步投產，主要承接客戶對非中國高階產能的需求。隨三地產能同步往高階產品
配置，研究部預估二代低介電布營收占比將由 2026 年的 27% 提升至 2027 
年的 51%，成為獲利成長的主要來源。研究部預估 2027 年營收 149.82 億元
（+52.4% YoY） ，並隨泰國產能開出及二代布占比提升呈逐季成長；毛利率升
至 54.8%，營益率達 45.7%，帶動稅後淨利 57.18 億元（+81.4% YoY），EPS 
9.30 元。

2H26 受惠二代低介電布需求放
量 、 漲價效益與良率改善 ， 富喬營
收與毛利率同步攀升，研究部預
期 3Q26、4Q26 營運將連續改寫
新高。

2027 年富喬成長動能將由漲價
轉 向 產 品 組 合 升 級 與 新 產 能 貢
獻 ， 隨台灣 、 東莞及泰國產能加速
轉向二代低介電布，研究部預估
二代布營收占比升至 51%，帶動 
EPS 成長至 9.30 元。

### Page 8

請參見末頁重要披露及免責聲明 8

投資建議 
研究部初次給予富喬買進投資評等，主要考量：(1) 石英布受鑽孔損耗與加
工良率限制 ， 導入時程遞延 ， 終端客戶轉回採用二代低介電布搭配高階樹脂的
架構，延長二代布產品週期，帶動富喬二代低介電布營收占比由 2025 年的 
7%，提升至 2026 年的 27% 與 2027 年的 51%；(2) 玻纖布織布機由豐田獨
家供應，目前新機交期已排至 2031 年，意味 2027 年以前產業新增供給主
要仰賴既有機台重新配置 ， 富喬可在無須大幅增加資本支出的情況下 ， 持續將
既有產能由 E 布轉向一代及二代低介電布，研究部推算二代布毛利率較一代
布高逾 10 個百分點，產品組合優化將推升毛利率由 2025 年的 27.6%，提
升至 2026 年的 44.7% 與 2027 年的 54.8%；(3) 東莞廠自 1Q27 起採台灣
供紗、當地織布模式，分批將既有產能轉作二代低介電布，泰國一期則規劃  
500 台織機，自 2H27 起逐步開出，同樣以低介電布為主要產品，並承接客
戶對非中國產能的需求 。綜上所述 ， 研究部預估富喬 2026、2027 年稅後 EPS 
分別為 5.13 元與 9.30 元，稅後純益分別年增 267.9% 與 81.4%。考量富
喬為日系龍頭以外少數具備紗、布垂直整合與自有配方 能力的供應商，以  
2027 年獲利為評價基礎，給予 20 倍本益比，目標價 185 元，初次給予買
進投資評等。

附錄 一 ： 評 等 之 標 準 
評等  定義  
強力買進     預估未來 6 個月內的絕對報酬超過 25%以上  
買進  預估未來 6 個月內的絕對報酬 10% 以上  
中立  預估未來 6 個月內的絕對報酬介於 10～-10%  
降低持股  預估未來 6 個月內的絕對報酬介於 (-10%)以下  
未評等  沒有足夠的基本資料判斷該公司評等

公 司 認 可 之 資 料來 源，並 基 於 特 定 日 期 所 做 之 判 斷，但 不 保 證 其 完 整 性 或 正 確 性，

提 供 專 屬 之 投 資 建 議。 文 中 所 載 資 訊 或 任 何 意 見，不 構 成 任 何 買 賣 有 價 證 券 或 其
他 投 資 標 的 之 要 約、 宣 傳 或 引 誘 等 事 項。 對 於 本 投 資 報 告 所 討 論 或 建 議 之 任 何 證
券 、 投 資 標 的 ， 或 文 中 所 討 論 或 建 議 之 投 資 策 略 ， 投 資 人 應 就 其 是 否 適 合 本 身 財
務 狀 況 與 投 資 條 件， 進 一 步 諮 詢 財 務 顧 問 的 意 見。本 投 資 報 告 之 內 容 取 材 自 據 信

確 性 作 出 任 何 陳 述 或 保 證。本 投 資 報 告 載 述 意 見 進 行 更 改 與 撤 回 並 不 另 行 通 知。

### Page 1

產業速報  
                                        2026 年 9 月 9 日

1 群益投顧 
ASML (ASML US) 與台積電 (2330 TT) 共同推動 High NA EUV 將自現行的 6 吋光罩，
轉移至更大的 12 吋 光 罩。 目標於 2031 年之前建立 12 吋 光 罩 試 產 線(pilot line) ， 並 於
2033 年之前實現微影系統全面就緒。台積電並表示自 2030 年 起 ， 將 在 先 進 製 程 量 產
中導入 ASML 的 High NA  EUV 。台積電與 ASML 共同發起 6 吋光罩轉移至 12 吋光
罩 的 規 格 轉 移 ， 其 戰 略 意 義 在 於 ， 製 訂 規 格 和 標 準 ， 確 保 台 積 電 技 術 領 先 地 位 。 因 此
對 台 積 電 的 投 資 評 等 Buy ，目標價 3,200 元。

研 究 員 ： 陳 俐 妍   
ly.c@capital.com.tw

ASML 與 台 積 電 共 同 推 動 High NA EUV 將 自 現 行 的 6 吋 光 罩 ， 轉 移 至 更 大
的 12 吋 光 罩 ：  
09/07/2026 ASML 與 台 積 電 於 BACUS 國 際 光 學 工 程 學 會(SPIE) 先 進 微 影 技
術 研 討 會 前 宣 布 發 起 產 業 合 作 ， 共 同 推 動High NA EUV 將 自 現 行 的 6 吋光
罩 ， 轉 移 至 更 大 的 12 吋 光 罩 ， 預 期 將 進 一 步 提 升 晶 圓 廠 的 生 產 力 、 降 低 晶
片 製 造 成 本 ， 並 消 除 需 要 2 張 6 吋 光 罩 在 High NA EUV 曝 光 時 進 行 拼 接 的
限 制，使 整 個 半 導 體 產 業 受 惠。此 轉 移 能 讓 先 進 晶 片 製 造 服 務 提 供 者 充 分 利
用 High NA EUV 的 優 勢 ， 更 提 升 未 來 世 代 先 進 晶 片 的 成 本 效 益 。 
目標於 2031 年 之 前 建 立 12 吋 光 罩 試 產 線(pilot line) ，並於 2033 年 之 前 實
現 微 影 系 統 全 面 就 緒 ， 以 支 援 先 進 製 程 量 產 。 台 積 電 並 表 示 自2030 年 起 ，
將 在 先 進 製 程 量 產 中 導 入 ASML 的 High NA 技 術 。  
12 吋光罩 使 High NA 效 率 提 升 ：  
現行 EUV 設備的 NA(Numerical Aperture ， NA) 為 0.33 ， High NA 則 提 高 到
0.55 。 NA 數 值 越 高 ， 解 析 度 也 越 高 、 曝 光 時 能 呈 現 的 細 節 愈 多 。ASML 資
料顯示 High NA 可 將 最 小 解 析 尺 寸 從 現 行 EUV 的約 13 奈 米 進 一 步 縮 小 到
8 奈 米 ， 也 就 是 能 在 晶 圓 上 印 出 更 細 微 的 電 路 圖 形 。 部 分 原 本 需 要2 次 甚 至
更多次曝光才能完成的圖形，也有機會改以單次曝光處理，降低製程複雜
度 、 缺 陷 風 險 與 生 產 周 期 。 
舉 例 來 說 ，0.33 NA EUV 機 台 ， 至 少 需 要 4 張 光 罩 、 分 多 次 曝 光 才 能 完 成
A14 、 A10 金 屬 層 圖 形 ；High NA EUV ， 只 要 1 張 光 罩 就 能 完 成 相 同 圖 形 。
但 解 析 度 提 高 ，High NA 的 光 學 設 計 讓 單 次 可 曝 光 範 圍 縮 小 到 原 本EUV 的
50% ， 製 作 大 顆 晶 片 時 可 能 必 須 分 成2 次 曝 光 ， 再 把 圖 形 拼 接 起 來 。 這 也 是
台積電與 ASML 此 次 推 動 12 吋 光 罩 的 原 因 ， 相 較 現 行 6 吋 光 罩 ， 放 大 光 罩
尺 寸 預 期 能 進 一 步 提 升 晶 圓 廠 生 產 力、降 低 晶 片 製 造 成 本，並 消 除 High NA
曝 光 時 的 拼 接 限 制 。 
台 積 電 預 計 2030 年 開 始 使 用 High NA  EUV ， 推估 High NA 將 會 在 A10 或
A10 以 下 的 先 進 製 程 採 用：  
台積電預計 2030 年 開 始 導入 High NA  EUV ，比 12 吋 光 罩 微 影 系 統 預 計 全
面就緒的 2033 年 更 早 。 這 代 表 台 積 電 初 期 導 入High NA 時 ， 仍 會 先 使 用 現
行 6 吋 光 罩 ， 之 後 再 隨 12 吋 光 罩 與 相 關 系 統 成 熟 逐 步 轉 換 。

### Page 2

產業速報  
                                        2026 年 9 月 9 日

2 群益投顧 
根 據 台 積 電 目 前 公 開 的 製 程 藍 圖 ，A13 與 A12 製 程 都 預 計 2029 年 量 產 。 其
中 A13 是 A14 平 台 的 延 伸 ，A12 則 將 超 級 電 軌(Super Power Rail) 背面供電
技術導入 A14 平 台 。 因 此 N2 、 A16 、 A14 、 A13 與 A12 都 早 於 台 積 電 預 計
2030 年 導 入 High NA ， 推 估 High NA 將 會 在 A10 或 A10 以 下 的 先 進 製 程 採
用。  
Intel (INTC US) 和 Samsung (005930 KS) 領 先 台 積 電 採 用 High NA EUV ，但
也 同 時 參 與 12 吋 光 罩 開 發 ：  
Intel 在 2024 年 宣 布 完 成 全 球 首 套 商 用 High NA EUV 設 備 的 組 裝 ；2026 年
High NA 已 經 正 式 進 入 量 產 ， 並 用 於 部 分 18A 製 程 層 。 是 目 前 High NA 導
入 進 度 最 快 的 晶 片 製 造 商 。Samsung 則預計 2028 年導入 DRAM 量 產 ； 台 積
電選擇在 2030 年 導 入 。Intel 和 Samsung 也 同 時 表 示 與 ASML 及 供 應 鏈 合
作 ， 推 動 未 來 轉 向 大 尺 寸 光 罩 。 
台 積 電 與 ASML 共 同 發 起 6 吋 光 罩 轉 移 至 12 吋 光 罩 的 規 格 轉 移，將 領 導 業
界 規 格 ：  
6 吋 光 罩 轉 移 至 12 吋光罩 的 這 項 升 級 非 單 純 的 尺 寸 放 大 ， 而 是 面 臨 生 態 系
與 物 理 極 限 的 多 重 挑 戰 。 整 體 供 應 鏈 重 構 ， 包 括 基 板 與 材 料 以 及 設 備 等 等 。
12 吋 光 罩 石 英 基 板 的 平 坦 度 、 缺 陷 密 度 與 熱 膨 脹 係 數 控 制 難 度 倍 增 。 製 程
設 備 如 薄 膜 沉 積、蝕 刻、清 洗 與 自 動 化 搬 運 設 備 皆 需 重 新 開 發。後 續 檢 測 與
量測極限檢測難度也增加，更大面積的圖形缺陷檢測（ Inspection ）與修補
技 術 需 要 突 破 。 在 12 吋 面 積 上 維 持 奈 米 級 的 對 位 精 度 與 線 寬 均 勻 性 極 為 困
難   
電 子 設 計 自 動 化 (EDA) 工 具 必 須 重 新 適 應 大 尺 寸 曝 光 視 場 與 資 料 處 理 。 12 
吋 光 罩 的 圖 形 檔 案 與 運 算 複 雜 度 將 大 幅 上 升 。 
台積電與 ASML 共 同 發 起 6 吋 光 罩 轉 移 至 12 吋 光 罩 的 規 格 轉 移，其 戰略意
義在於 ，台 積 電 為 全 球 最 大 晶 圓 代 工 廠，為 先 進 製 程 最 大 的 供 應 商，可 以 領
導 光 罩 基 底 供 應 商 、 光 罩 檢 測 設 備 商， 甚 至 EDA 廠 等 整 個 生 態 系 ，除 共同
分攤 ， 跨 越 升 級 12 吋 光 罩 的 龐 大 研 發 成 本外 ， 製 訂 規 格 和 標 準 ， 確 保 台 積
電 技 術 領 先 地 位 。因 此 對 台 積 電 的投 資 評 等 Buy ， 目 標 價 3,200 元。

3 群益投顧 
  【 投 資 評 等 說 明 】 
  評等  定義  
  強力買進 (Strong Buy)  首 次 評 等 潛 在 上 漲 空 間≥ 35%  
  買進 (Buy)  15%≤ 首 次 評 等 潛 在 上 漲 空 間<35%  
  區間操作 (Trading Buy)  5%≤ 首 次 評 等 潛 在 上 漲 空 間<15%

中立 (Neutral)  
無 法 由 基 本 面 給 予 投 資 評 等 
預 期 近 期 股 價 將 處 於 盤 整 
建 議 降 低 持 股

【 投 資 評 等 調 整 機 制 】 
  投 資 評 等 係「 首 次 」給 予 特 定 個 股 投 資 評 等 時，其 前 一 日 收 盤 價 相 較3 個月
目 標 價 之 潛 在 上 漲 空 間 計 算 而 得 。 個 股 投 資 評 等 分 為四 個 等 級 ， 定 義 如 上 。
爾 後 的 投 資 評 等係 依 循 「 首 次 評 等 」 ， 直 到 停 止 推 薦 。  
「 強 力 買 進 」、「 買 進 」及 「 區 間 操 作 」均有 upside 目 標 價 。差 別 僅 在 於 ，
首 次 評 等 時 潛 在 報 酬 率 不 同 。 「 中 立 」 則 無 目 標 價 。 
一 旦 我 們 給 予 特 定 個 股「 強 力 買 進 」、「 買 進 」或「 區 間 操 作 」之 投 資 評 等，
就 是 責 任 的 開 始，爾 後 將 透 過 各 式 研 究 報 告 作 定 期 性、持 續 性 追 蹤 基 本 面 及
股 價 變 化 ， 直 到 停 止 推 薦 。 
停 止 推 薦 情 境 ： 
1.  達 目 標 價 。 
2.  雖 未 達 原 訂 目 標 價 ， 但 檢 視 基 本 面 、 訊 息 面 、 籌 碼 面 等 多 方 訊 息 ， 研
判 股 價 上 漲 空 間 已 然 有 限 ， 將 適 時 出 具 降 評 報 告 。 
3.  推 薦 後 股 價 不 漲 反 跌 。

【免責聲明】  
  本 研 究 報 告 僅 提 供 予 特 定 人 之 客 戶 作 為 參 考 資 料「 非 經 同 意 不 得 轉 載 」 。我
們 並 不 確 保 此 資 訊 的 完 整 性 與 正 確 性，投 資 人 應 了 解，報 告 中 有 關 未 來 預 測
之 陳 述 可 能 不 會 實 現，因 而 不 應 被 依 賴。而 且 此 報 告 並 非 根 據 特 定 投 資 目 的
或 依 預 定 對 象 之 財 務 狀 況 所 撰 寫 出 來 的，因 此，此 研 究 報 告 的 目 的，既 非 對
投 資 人 於 買 賣 證 券、選 擇 權、期 貨 或 其 他 證 券 相 關 之 衍 生 性 商 品 提 供 詢 價 服
務，亦 非 作 為 進 行 交 易 的 要 約。投 資 人 應 注 意 到 相 關 證 券 之 價 值 及 收 益，可
能會有無預警地上升或下降，產生投資回報金額可能比原始投資來得少的
情形。

### Page 1

Taiwan Information Technology

What’s new: We provide an update on Winway’s business outlook.

What’s the impact: Marked revenue upside, but a more conservative 
gross margin outlook. We raise our 3Q26 revenue forecast by 9.3% to 
TWD5.0bn (+177.8% YoY) to reflect the stronger-than-expected execution 
of capacity expansion at Renwu Phase 1. Management guided for flat QoQ 
revenue in 4Q26, due to: 1) seasonality; 2) existing capacity being nearly 
fully utilised, with additional capacity scheduled to come online in 1H27; 
and 3) potential revenue recognition delays into 1H27 to accommodate 
customers’ schedules. On profitability, we lower our 3Q26E gross margin 
by 1.8pp, as we expect higher revenue contribution from probe cards and 
incremental depreciation from the new plant to dilute margins. Overall, for 
2026E and 2027E, we raise our revenue by 3.2% and 0.9%, while lowering 
our gross margin by 1.2pp and 1.7pp, respectively.

Probe card share concerns appear overdone. In response to concerns 
over probe card order losses, we believe the expanding TAM will attract 
competitors; the key differentiator is whether suppliers can scale capacity 
to meet customers’ demands. Winway does not expect its probe card share 
at its largest customer to decline and remains confident in maintaining its 
market-leading position. Its Micro-Electro-Mechanical Systems (MEMS) 
probe card supply agreement with Technoprobe (TPRO IM, Not Rated) 
remains in place, which should support its technology competitiveness.

Socket capacity expansion remains on track. Winway doubled its socket 
capacity YoY in 2026 and remains on track to reach monthly pogo-pin 
capacity of 14m units by 1H27, per management. We estimate monthly 
capacity to grow further to 16-18m units by end-2027. Management’s long-
term target is to raise its pogo-pin self-sufficiency ratio to 50-60%, which 
should support gross margin expansion through greater vertical integration.

What we recommend: We slightly raise revenue top-line but lower our 
2026-28E EPS by 1-5% to factor in conservative gross margin 
assumptions. We reaffirm our Buy (1) call, with 12M TP of TWD10,000, 
based on a PER of 50x, applied to our 2027E forward EPS. Our 2026-28E 
EPS CAGR of 100% makes our target PER undemanding, given that the 
resulting PEG ratio is 0.5x. Key downside risks: intensifying competition.

How we differ: Our revised 2026-27E EPS are now roughly in line with 
consensus, reflecting a more prudent gross margin. While market noise 
persists, we see no structural threat to Winway's leading position in sockets 
and probe cards.

21 September 2026

Capacity ramp drives revenue upside

➢ Revenue upside with more prudent gross margin assumptions 
➢ Probe card position intact; socket expansion on track 
➢ Reaffirming Buy (1) rating with an unchanged 12M TP of TWD10,000

Company profile 
Founded in Kaohsiung in 2001, WinWay has evolved from an optoelectronic test-fixture supplier into a 
leading logic test-interface provider. Its portfolio includes test sockets, MEMS probe cards and contact 
elements. Growth is driven by increasing AI/HPC testing complexity, GPU and ASIC design wins, 
expanding SLT adoption and next-generation HyperSocket solutions.

### Page 1

Formosa Petrochemical (6505.TW)
Upgrade to Buy: Superior Margin on Tanker Fleet + High Export Ratio
CITI'S TAKE 
We upgrade FPCC from Neutral to Buy reflecting its unique exposure with 
in-house VLCC shipping fleet covering ~60-70% of oil transportation (least 
impacted by recent spot freight rate spike) and highest diesel export ratio 
( ~85% vs. 60% for S-Oil/SKI and 5-10% for TOP/SPRC). We see manageable 
impact from CPC’s pump price freeze (minimal margin squeeze at ~$80/bbl 
Dubai) while FPCC is also a beneficiary of any potential US diesel export 
restriction (Iink). We expect FPCC to offer the highest 26-27E dividend yield 
( ~ 7-8%) among 4 Formosa sisters and a premium valuation seems justified 
vs. Asian peers. We think oil product tightness might take longer to resolve 
given extensive Russia refinery outages. New TP at NT$108 (from NT$40). 
Quantifying impact of domestic price cap — Taiwan’s CPC indicates its retail price 
absorption reached NT$22bn cumulative YTD (Fig.1) vs. NT$17bn in end-Jun and we 
estimate ~NT$7bn in Sep alone (similar to Mar peak). FPCC has 22% Taiwan market 
share and 2025 domestic gasoline & diesel volume was 35k/17k b/d or 12% of CDU 
runs subject to price control (vs. ~18% for S-Oil/SKI, 35% for Thai peers). Assuming 
oil price stays at $100 until end-4Q, we estimate 2H26 domestic forgone EBIT could 
be similar to 1H ( ~NT$5bn) or ~US$2/bbl overall GRM drag. But this was more than 
compensated by SG product crack strength with 2025 gasoline/diesel/jet export 
ratios of 59%/86%/99%, respectively.   
Buffered from current $20+ spot VLCC rate — FPCC is one of the very few refiners 
that owns vessels (through 20% stake in Formosa Plastics Marine that has 6 VLCCs). 
We expect it could enjoy stable freight cost under LT time chartering. We also see 
lower concern on crude sourcing from SoH disruption in 3Q noting Jul/Aug refinery 
runs were 88%/92%. But we see pullback into 4Q (mid-70%) on maintenance. 
Improved olefin competitiveness from 2027 — We expect FPCC could raise cracker 
utilisation next year (33% average since 2Q) after partial conversion to ethane feed 
(up to ~400kt pa usage or ~20% of output) with minimal capex. 
Implications — Our revised TP of NT$108 is based on historical peak 2.3x P/BV (core 
book) at ~50% premium to Korean peer S-Oil given limited free float and MTM for 
affiliates (its 7.9% stake in Nanya Tech is worth NT$14/share based on market price). 
Our sensitivity analysis suggests a $5 diesel crack rise vs. our mid-$30/bbl base 
case will raise FY27E EPS by ~10%.

In Taiwan, state-owned Chinese Petroleum Corp (CPC) announces retail price
adjustment on a weekly basis based on government’s fuel pricing formula (linked to
70% Dubai / 30% Brent crude basket adjusted for exchange rate) but with higher
absorption amount as oil goes higher. FPCC tends to follow suit. CPC’s domestic
price absorption was minimal in Jun-Jul’26 when Dubai price falls to ~$80/bbl or

below. However, there was a price freeze last week despite crude basket surged
another $10/bbl WoW to $126/bbl.

### Page 7

Formosa Petrochemical (6505.TW)
25 September 2026 Citi Research
7
Formosa Petrochemical
Company description
Formosa Petrochemical (FPCC) is part of the Formosa Group in Taiwan. It
owns a 540,000 b/d refinery, 2.9m tonnes of ethylene capacity from three
naphtha crackers, and a 2,800MW cogeneration power plant. It was set up
when the Taiwan government approved expansion of the private sector into
the oil refining and upstream chemical business.
Investment strategy

We rate Formosa Petrochemical stock a Buy. Despite a likely gradual GRM
normalisation ahead (dependent on Middle East / Russia supply disruptions),
we expect oil product tightness might take longer to resolve vs. crude oil
market. Its gasoline/diesel export ratio was the highest among Asian peers,
which should more than buffer margin squeeze from domestic pump control.
FPCC was the least impacted by recent spot freight rate spike given in-house
VLCC shipping fleet. While we remain concerned about an L-shaped ethylene

cycle in the next 2-3 years, FPCC could enjoy better cost competitiveness as
~20% of olefin cracker feedstock will shift to ethane from early-2027 with
minimal capex. The LT refining outlook could remain solid due to permanent
closures in the US/Europe. Healthy dividend yield (likely to be the highest
among 4 Formosa sisters in FY26E), stable utility earnings and the stock's
relatively low free float (15%) vs. its peers could provide further support.

Valuation
Our NT$108 target price for Formosa Petrochemical is based on historical
peak-cycle 2.3x FY27E P/BV with adjusted book value excluding valuation
gain of affiliates, ~50% premium to Korean peer S-Oil implied from our
TP given limited free float and MTM for affiliates. We use P/BV as our
valuation methodology due to the asset-heavy nature of the business, and
factor in its scarcity premium vs, regional peers as the only energy play in
Taiwan.

Risks
Key downside risks that could impede the shares from reaching our target
price include: 1) weaker-than-expected oil and/or chemical demand; and 2)
Taiwan government intervention on CPC's oil products pricing mechanism.
Key upside risks are: 1) extended refinery outages in the Middle East / Russia,
2) stronger global oil demand growth; 3) robust chemical re-stocking in
China; and 4) a short-term spike in refining margins on any unscheduled
shutdowns or for weather-related reasons (e.g., cold winter).

Prepared for Kevin Lu

Guide to Citi Research Fundamental Research Investment Ratings:
Citi Research stock recommendations include an investment rating and an optional risk rating to highlight high risk stocks.

Risk rating takes into account both price volatility and fundamental criteria. Stocks will either have no risk rating or a High risk
rating assigned.
Investment Ratings: Citi Research investment ratings are Buy, Neutral and Sell. Our ratings are a function of analyst
expectations of expected total return ("ETR") and risk. ETR is the sum of the forecast price appreciation (or depreciation) plus
the dividend yield for a stock within the next 12 months.  The target price is based on a 12 month time horizon. The Investment

rating definitions are: Buy (1) ETR of 15% or more or 25% or more for High risk stocks; and Sell (3) for negative ETR. Any covered
stock not assigned a Buy or a Sell is a Neutral (2). For stocks rated Neutral (2), if an analyst believes that there are insufficient
valuation drivers and/or investment catalysts to derive a positive or negative investment view, they may elect with the approval
of Citi Research management not to assign a target price and, thus, not derive an ETR. Citi Research may suspend its rating

and target price and assign “Rating Suspended” status for regulatory and/or internal policy reasons. Citi Research may also
suspend its rating and target price and assign "Under Review" status for other exceptional circumstances (e.g. lack of
information critical to the analyst's thesis, trading suspension) affecting the company and/or trading in the company's
securities. In both such situations, the rating and target price will show as “—“ and “-“ respectively in the rating history price

chart. Prior to 11 April 2022 Citi Research assigned “Under Review” status to both situations and prior to 11 Nov 2020 only in
exceptional circumstances. As soon as practically possible, the analyst will publish a note re-establishing a rating and
investment thesis. Investment ratings are determined by the ranges described above at the time of initiation of coverage, a
change in investment and/or risk rating, or a change in target price (subject to limited management discretion). At times, the

expected total returns may fall outside of these ranges because of market price movements and/or other short-term volatility
or trading patterns. Such interim deviations will be permitted but will become subject to review by Research Management.
Your decision to buy or sell a security should be based upon your personal investment objectives and should be made only
after evaluating the stock's expected performance and risk.
Catalyst Watch/Short Term Views (“STV”) Ratings Disclosure:

Catalyst Watch and STV Upside/Downside calls: Citi Research may also include a Catalyst Watch or STV Upside or Downside
call to indicate the analyst expects the share price to rise (fall) in absolute terms over a specified period of 30 or 90 days in
reaction to one or more specific near-term catalysts or events impacting the company or the market. A Catalyst Watch will be
published when Analyst confidence is high that an impact to share price will occur; it will be a STV when confidence level is

moderate. A Catalyst Watch or STV Upside/Downside call will automatically expire at the end of the specified 30/90 day
period. The Catalyst Watch will also be automatically removed if share price performance (calculated at market close) exceeds
15% against the direction of the call (unless over-ridden by the analyst). The analyst may also remove a Catalyst Watch or STV
call prior to the end of the specified period in a published research note. A Catalyst Watch/STV Upside or Downside call may be

our ratings distribution table for our Catalyst Watch/STV Upside/Downside rating system. However, we reiterate that we do
not consider No View to be a recommendation. For all Catalyst Watch/STV Upside/Downside calls, risk exists that the
catalyst(s) and associated share-price movement will not materialize as expected.

AVC (3017.TW): Liquid cooling rising adoption along with new AI servers 
across GPU and ASIC to drive sequential monthly revenues growth; Buy
25 September 2026 | 7:51PM HKT
3-month revenues preview: We expect AVC’s monthly revenues to continue 
sequential growth through Dec 2026, leading 3Q26 / 4Q26 revenues to grow at 
+18% / +27% QoQ, driven by (1) new AI server racks ramp up, driving overall liquid 
cooling penetration rate. As we highlighted in June (report link), we expect AI server 
rack (NVL72-equivalent) shipment (incl. Nvidia and AMD) to reach 55k/ 105k/ 163k 
in 2026-28E, increasing by 16% / 20% in 2027 / 28E, reflecting our positive view on 
AI server racks, (2) its major ASIC AI server customer’s new model ramp up, driving its 
air-cooling and liquid-cooling components, along with a rising liquid cooling 
penetration rate, (3) its customer base expansion to a new ASIC AI server, to further 
drive its liquid cooling components in 4Q26, and (4) increasing adoption rate of 
ultra-thin VC in consumer electronics to fit the lightweight design and major 
customer’s foldable phone model. We expect AVC’s net income to grow at a +39% 
CAGR in 2026-28E, riding on the strong AI spending, a growing liquid cooling 
penetration rate, and AVC’s leading market position in total solutions (air cooling, 
liquid cooling with various components / systems). Maintain Buy.

update our target P/E multiple to 30.3x 2027E EPS (vs. 28.2x previously), which is still 
derived from PC/server peers’ EPS growth vs. P/E multiple correlation. Our target P/E 
multiple is above AVC’s historical average +1stv. forward trading P/E, reflecting our 
positive view on AVC riding on the rising penetration rate of liquid-cooled AI servers. 
With our updated target P/E and earnings estimates, we raise our 12-month target price 
to NT$4,513 (from NT$3,986). Maintain Buy.

### Page 3

Price Target Risks and Methodology - AVC 
We are Buy-rated on AVC. Our 12-month target price for AVC is based on a near-term 
P/E, consistent with our Taiwan Technology coverage. We base our target price of 
NT$4,513 on a target P/E multiple of 30.3x applied to our forward-year EPS (2027E). 
Our target P/E is derived from the correlation between P/E and EPS growth of its peers 
in technology hardware.  
Key downside risks: (1) slower-than-expected adoption of liquid cooling; (2) 
lower-than-expected demand for AI servers; (3) fiercer-than-expected competition; 
and (4) a slower-than-expected recovery in general servers.

Ratings, coverage universe and related definitions

on an Investment List is determined by a stock’s total return potential relative to its coverage universe. Any stock not assigned as a Buy or a Sell on an
Investment List with an active rating (i.e., a  stock that is not Rating Suspended, Not Rated, Early-Stage Biotech, Coverage Suspended or Not Covered), is

deemed Neutral. Each region manages Regional Conviction Lists, which are selected from Buy rated stocks on the respective region’s Investment Lists
and represent investment recommendations focused on the size of the total return potential and/or the likelihood of the realization of the return across
their respective areas of coverage.  The addition or removal of stocks from such Conviction Lists are managed by the Investment Review Committee or
other designated committee in each respective region and do not represent a change in the analysts’ investment rating for such stocks.

Total return potential represents the upside or downside differential between the current share price and the price target, including all paid or
anticipated dividends, expected during the time horizon associated with the price target. Price targets are required for all covered stocks. The total
return potential, price target and associated time horizon are stated in each report adding or reiterating an Investment List membership.

Compal (2324.TW): AI servers to fuel 4Q26E/1Q27E growth; Aug miss; 
Neutral
27 September 2026 | 6:06PM HKT
3-month revenues preview: We expect sequential revenue growth in Sep-Dec, 
considering the company’s rising AI server shipments, AI server capacity expansion, 
and PC product mix upgrade towards high end model (e.g. AI PC). Overall, we expect 
Compal’s 3Q / 4Q26E revenues to grow at 1% / 16% QoQ, riding on AI server racks 
ramp up and the company’s expansion from motherboard (L6) to system (L10 / L11). 
We remain positive on Compal’s business diversification, supported by global 
capacity expansion in the US, Taiwan, Vietnam and China, while consumer 
electronics still accounts for the majority of the company’s total revenue, for which 
demand could be capped by rising raw material costs (e.g., memory, CPU). Maintain 
Neutral on fair valuation.

Compal’s Aug revenue was up 15% YoY / down 17% MoM to NT$67bn, or 20% lower 
than our estimate, reflecting PC shipments decline. Aug PC shipments was down 22% 
MoM to 1.4m units, reflecting the impact of 1H26 demand pull-in amid the rising 
memory cost and weak consumer electronics demand.

Earnings revision: Factoring in Compal’s Aug revenue, we cut our 2026-28E net

Price Target Risks and Methodology - Compal 
We are Neutral-rated on Compal with a 12-month target price of NT$46.1. Our target 
price is based on 14.7x 2027E P/E, which is derived from PC/server peers’ EPS growth 
vs. P/E multiple correlation. We see EPS growth and market re-rating for ODM 
companies as major factors for the stock’s performance.  
Key upside/downside risks: 1) stronger-/weaker-than-expected PC market recovery; 
2) faster-/slower-than-expected AI server ramp-up; and 3) 
stronger/weaker-than-expected demand for tablets.

Ratings, coverage universe and related definitions
Buy (B), Neutral (N), Sell (S) Analysts recommend stocks as Buys or Sells for inclusion on various regional Investment Lists. Being assigned a Buy or Sell
on an Investment List is determined by a stock’s total return potential relative to its coverage universe. Any stock not assigned as a Buy or a Sell on an
Investment List with an active rating (i.e., a  stock that is not Rating Suspended, Not Rated, Early-Stage Biotech, Coverage Suspended or Not Covered), is

deemed Neutral. Each region manages Regional Conviction Lists, which are selected from Buy rated stocks on the respective region’s Investment Lists
and represent investment recommendations focused on the size of the total return potential and/or the likelihood of the realization of the return across
their respective areas of coverage.  The addition or removal of stocks from such Conviction Lists are managed by the Investment Review Committee or
other designated committee in each respective region and do not represent a change in the analysts’ investment rating for such stocks.

Total return potential represents the upside or downside differential between the current share price and the price target, including all paid or
anticipated dividends, expected during the time horizon associated with the price target. Price targets are required for all covered stocks. The total
return potential, price target and associated time horizon are stated in each report adding or reiterating an Investment List membership.

### Page 1

Aspeed

Growth profile remains robust into 2027; 
reiterate Buy

Reiterate Rating: BUY | PO: 26,000 TWD | Price: 19,835 TWD

Looming price hike and smoother supply 
Aspeed’s (see report) 4Q26 revenue guidance in our view should incorporate 20% price 
hike across the board including AST2700 BMC (priced at ~US$30 after the hike), mainly 
reflecting the cost inflation from OSAT (outsourced semiconductor assembly/testing) 
vendor. Yet with incremental supply secured across foundry, OSAT, substrate and glass 
cloth, the firm expects a normalized DoI (days of inventory) level of ~12 weeks from 
2Q27.   
Sustained leadership with value-add in security 
Management continues to underscore around 2-year technology lead vs its main 
competitor in the merchant market, while the adoption of Caliptra 2.2 standard in 
AST2750 BMC further enhanced its value-add in network security. We deem this a 
positive to (see report) eMemory given the share gain in PUF business against its 
counterpart in US, from which Aspeed adopts Caliptra 1.1 in AST2700 BMC.   
Rosy demand growth outlook for 2027 
Looking into 2027, Aspeed cites 50-60% growth for BMC applied to GPU and ASIC 
markets, while there could be 1) an even stronger growth in Agentic AI (artificial 
intelligence); and 2) 6-10% YoY in general server (w.o. Agentic AI in our view). Given such 
demand strength, the company may provide an updated version of the BMC TAM (total 
addressable market) by end-2026.   
Flag recent positives from the industry; re-it. Buy 
Recently we see positives to server CPU (central processing unit) supply chain from (see 
reports) Meta’s Muse and expert call takeaways which cite continuous share gain by 
ARM-based solutions. Reiterate Buy on Aspeed given its unrivalled position in a 
structurally growing market.

Company Sector 
Semiconductors

Company Description 
Aspeed was founded in 2004 as a fabless company, 
focusing on the design of baseboard management 
controller (BMC), which could be used in remote servers. 
Aspeed has 70+% market share in BMC, while it also offers 
other products including PC/AV extension IC and digital 
image processors.

Investment Rationale 
We rate Aspeed as Buy, as we anticipate stable growth in 
the regular server market into 2026-27, along with its 
growing exposure to AI server market. Outside of server 
BMC, we also like its increasing traction in new products 
(mini-BMC, root of trust, etc.) in the server area. Further, we 
see stronger growth in non-server business led mainly by 
Cupola 360.

### Page 4

Price objective basis & risk 
Aspeed (XLKMF) 
Our DCF-based PO is NT$26,000, and we believe the valuation is justified by (1) the 
firm's high market share in the server BMC market, (2) secular growth in overall server 
market, despite near-term ebbs and flows of orders and shipments, (3) nearly 60% 
earnings CAGR in 2026-28E, and (4) about NT$215 net cash/share on hand, we assume 
WACC of 7% and a terminal growth rate of 3%.

Upside risk to our PO are 
(1) stronger-than-expected operating leverage on stronger revenue growth, and (2) 
faster progress in ramping up non-BMC product lines including Cupola 360.

Downside risks to our PO are 
(1) softer server (thus BMC) demand due to macro uncertainties, or pressured by trade 
conflict 
(2) market-share loss in BMC to current/potential competitors such as Nuvoton 
(3) customers' market-share loss in ones (such as HP and Dell) to which Aspeed has less 
exposure.

ab 28 September 2026
Global Research
Nan Ya Plastics
Buy on CCL price hikes and AI-led mix upgrade
Assume coverage with a  Buy and a higher price target of NT$300.00
We assume coverage of Nan Ya Plastics (NYP)  with a Buy and PT of NT$300.  The share
price is up c310% YTD, while Nan Ya PCB (NYPCB),  61% owned by NYP, is up c420%
YTD.  We believe most of NYP's share price appreciation has been driven by the value of
its listed holdings rather than its core Copper Clad Laminate (CCL) business. We

continue to flag earnings upside potential from CCL, driven by ASP increases of
90%/85% in 2026/27E and operating margin expansion from 4.1% in 2025 to
23%/30% in 2026/27E (H126 at c17%). Key drivers include supply led price hikes, a
richer AI product mix and NYP's integrated CCL value chain. In petrochemicals, we
expect the MEG-ethylene spread to turn positive in Q326, with 2026-27E spreads aided
by geopolitical supply tightness. Our 2026/27E earnings are 15%/23% above

Bloomberg consensus. Further upstream tightness and high-end AI CCL order wins are
the upside catalysts.
CCL ASP up 90%/85% in 2026/27E
We expect CCL ASP to rise 90%/85% YoY in 2026/27E:  c80%/55% from supply led
price hikes (see UBS  Tech team's CCL sector report) and c10%/30% from product mix
upgrades. Conventional CCL prices should be supported by AI capacity crowding out
commodity grades, higher material intensity and limited near-term upstream additions.

Industry CCL prices are up 100% in 2026 YTD;  part should flow into 2027E as contracts
reset. We expect M6+ revenue contribution to rise from 8% of CCL revenue in Q326 to
25% in 2027E with ASPs 3-10x higher than conventional FR4 products.
Vertical integration and ASP increase to support 23-30% CCL OPM
NYP's CCL chain covers  glass cloth, epoxy resin and copper foil. We view epoxy resin and
copper foil as fully self-supplied and most glass cloth as internally sourced. This should

support value capture as CCL prices rise. Based on historical sensitivity, we estimate that
a US$1.00 increase in CCL ASP would translate to an incremental US$0.45/0.37 per
piece increase in operating profit in 2026/27E. As a result, we forecast CCL OPM of
23%/30% in 2026/27. In-house material R&D could accelerate AI CCL qualification,
while the partnership with Nittobo would secure Gen2 low-Dk glass cloth and support
the ramp of high-end products.

Valuation:  new price target of NT$300.00
We value NYP using SOTP:  the UBS PT of NT$1,150 for NYPCB; 19x 2027E EV/EBITDA
for non-PCB electronic materials (CCL and upstream); 10x 2027E EV/EBITDA for
petchem (chemicals, plastics and polyester), all in line with peers. Listed stakes (FPCC,
FPC, FCFC, NYT, Mai Liao Power and others)  at UBS's PTs, market values, or peer
multiples. After a 10% HoldCo discount, our PT is NT$300.00, implying 15x 2027E PE,

0.3x PEG and 4.4x P/BV.

Thesis Map     UBS                    Research     THESIS MAP a guide to our thinking and what´s where in this report
Q: Will  CCL and electronic materials (EM) become the  key profit drivers?
Yes. We expect EM revenue to rise from 46% in 2025 to 59%/69% in 2026/27E, and to account for
nearly all EBIT. Within EM, CCL would be the growth engine, up from 24% of EM revenue  in 2025 to

31%/43% in 2026/27E.
Q: Will the conventional CCL upcycle last through 2027?
Yes. UBS's Tech team expects conventional CCL prices and profits to keep rising in 2027E on: 1) high-
end AI grades taking  capacity from commodity CCL;  2) higher material intensity  in AI-CCL lifting
upstream demand; and 3) tight weaving capacity limiting near-term capacity additions.
Q: Can NYP achieve higher volume in AI-CCL
Yes. Vertical integration would likely help "recipe" changes and customer qualification. The Nittobo

tie-up should secure low-Dk glass and support high-end ramp.
We assume coverage of NYP with a Buy rating and a PT of NT$300.00. The share price is up c310%
YTD, while NYPCB, 61%-owned by NYP, is up c420% YTD. We think most of the share price
movement reflects its listed holdings rather than a full re-rating of the core CCL business. We
continue to expect earnings upside from CCL ASP growth of 90%/85% and CCL OPM expansion
from 4.1% in 2025 to 23%/30% in 2026/27E (H126 at c17%) , driven by supply led price hikes, a

richer AI mix and its integrated CCL value chain. For petchem, we expect the MEG-ethylene spread to
turn positive in Q326E, with 2026/27E margins supported by geopolitical led supply tightness.
Further upstream tightness and high-end AI CCL order wins are the key upside catalysts.
1) Conventional CCL prices are up nearly 100% YTD vs. 2025;  hikes continue to occur on a monthly
basis. 2) CCL and glass cloth are running at close to full capacity (c90% utilisation). An increase in

capacity  of 6.2m pcs/month in Q326 to 6.5m by Q426; 6.7-7.0m by end-2027 according to the
company. 3) M7 and M8 low-/ultra low-loss CCL products are now listed on the company's website
(updated in September 2026). 4) The November 2025 partnership with Nittobo should help the
company secure next-gen low-Dk glass yarn supply  for high-end AI CCL.
NYP is trading at 12x 2027E PE and 3.6x 2027E P/BV, significantly below EM peers at 36x 2027E PE
and 8.2x P/BV.  Our PT of NT$300.00 implies 15x 2027E PE and 4.4x 2027E P/BV. We believe the gap

reflects concerns about petchem, rather than improved CCL profitability.

Nan Ya Plastics one of the core companies of Taiwan's Formosa Group. Its businesses span electronic
materials, chemicals, plastics processing and polyester. NYP is one of the largest CCL producers, with

a vertically integrated CCL value chain, supplying CCL to its 61%-owned subsidiary Nan Ya PCB and
other printed circuit board (PCB) makers. It owns 29% of Nan Ya Tech, the only Taiwan-based
dynamic random access memory (DRAM) brand. It also owns 23% of Formosa Petrochemical.

### Page 3

Nan Ya Plastics 28 September 2026 ab 3
Nan Ya Plastics UBS Research
NYP CCL: a vertically integrated traditional CCL leader; AI
CCL ramping
Copper foil, epoxy resin and glass fibre are the three major materials for CCL, accounting
for 42%, 26% and 19% of conventional CCL costs, respectively, according to
Trendforce. As CCL migrates to higher end AI grades, we expect glass fibre and specialty
resin to take a larger share of total BOM cost, mainly driven by higher value low-Dk glass

and low-loss resin.
NYP has a highly vertically integrated CCL supply chain with in-house production
spanning glass yarn, glass fibre, epoxy resin, copper foil, and CCL. We believe vertical
integration differentiates NYP from other CCL companies given: 1) Provides supply
security: during periods of tight raw material supply, NYP can rely on internal key
material capacity, thereby reducing procurement risk and supporting stable production.
2) Faster customer qualification : with key upstream materials produced in-house,

NYP can adjust formulations and specifications more quickly to meet customer-specific
performance requirements during high-end CCL qualification. 3) Better cost control:
internal sourcing allows NYP to capture more value across the supply chain.

AI bandwidth growth boosts high-end CCL demand
As AI chips become more powerful ( Blackwell to NVIDIA's Rubin and Vera Rubin ), the
chips need to send more data at much faster speeds. The faster the data travels, the
easier it is for the signal to weaken or lose quality.  This means AI servers need better

circuit board materials to keep data moving quickly and reliably. High-end CCL, such as
M8 and M9, reduces this signal loss and therefore is becoming increasingly important as
AI computing power and data speeds continue to rise.

Vertical integration supports higher OPM
We compared CCL profitability with KBL (Kingboard Laminates, 1888.HK; covered by
UBS analyst Edward Liu), NYP and ITEQ (ITEQ Corporation, 6213.TW; Not Covered) and

note that vertical integration appears to support a higher margin. Fully integrated across
the three key CCL upstream materials, KBL delivered a 26% OPM in H126, followed by
17% for NYP,   and 11% for ITEQ which primarily sources upstream materials externally.
While product mix differs across peers, we believe companies with vertical integrated
supply chains are able to deliver better cost controls for raw materials and thus capture
higher value across the supply chain, particularly as upstream prices rise.

Supply-driven price upcycle to continue
Despite lukewarm consumer electronics demand, conventional CCL remains in a strong
pricing upcycle. The company has said that prices for mid and low-end CCL continue to
rise 5-10% per month, while certain grades are recording monthly increases of 10-15%.

The current supply shortfall reflects a combination of the following:
1) Capacity migration to high-end AI materials. As industry companies increasingly
prioritise AI-related products, a growing portion of CCL capacity is being reallocated
from conventional grades. High-end materials also require significantly longer
processing times and lower throughput, effectively reducing industry-wide supply of
traditional CCL.
2) Rising material intensity and order spillover. Increasing PCB complexity, including

higher layer counts in AI servers and networking equipment, is structurally raising CCL
consumption per unit. Concurrently, leading high-end CCL suppliers are focusing on
premium products, resulting in order spillover into the conventional CCL market and
tightening supply further.
3) Persistent upstream bottlenecks.  Tight key raw material supply, particularly for
glass fibre cloth and copper foil, continues to constrain effective capacity additions. The

limited availability of electronic grade glass cloth, long lead times for weaving
equipment and emerging shortages in high-end copper foil have extended the supply
crunch across the broader CCL value chain.
As the AI cycle continues, UBS's Tech team expects industry supply-demand conditions
to remain tight, at least through 2027E as capacity expansion requires multiple years to
materialise and is unlikely to keep pace with demand growth, in our view. As a result, we

expect conventional CCL pricing to remain on an upward trajectory, supporting further
margin expansion across the traditional CCL value chain.

Supply-driven price hikes to drive 80%/55% ASP growth in 2026/27E
Conventional CCL pricing has entered a sharp supply-driven upcycle. According to

TPCA,  Kingboard's FR4 price hikes exceeded 50% cumulatively by July, while local media
estimated YTD increases had exceeded 100% by end-August following further hikes.
NYP also confirmed 100% YTD price hikes for a number of conventional CCL products.
According to the Panasonic Industry Official Price Announcement , Panasonic
announced  three rounds of price increases for general glass-epoxy CCL in 2026 of 30%,
20% and 30%, equivalent to 103% on a compounded basis.

NYP management noted that the 26Q4 pricing upcycle remains intact, with CCL prices
rising 5-10% monthly, while some grades have increased more than 100% cumulatively
since 2025. KBL also confirmed that conventional CCL prices have more than doubled
versus end-2025. KBL raised prices twice in April and accelerated monthly increases to
15% in June and July.
Historically, NYP's CCL ASP has moved in the same direction as KBL. Based on the UBS
Tech team's sector view, we forecast CCL ASP price increases of 80%/55% in 2026/27,

driven purely by pricing. We flag the announced industry price hikes of up to 100% are
not immediately fully reflected in realised ASP, as implementation varies by customer and
often requires contract renewal or price renegotiation.

EMC anticipates undersupply to continue in the next two years
At the UBS Taiwan Summit, EMC (2383.TW, Not Covered) anticipated further price hikes
in H226 based on rising component costs, but that the magnitude of the increase would
likely be less than in H126. Management noted equipment lead time currently is one to
two years and that high-end CCL demand has not slowed. It thus anticipates

undersupply to continue in the next two years.
High-end CCL: next-gen AI rack architecture to lift CCL content and ASP
High-end AI CCL is mainly used in AI server compute boards, switch boards and
midplane/backplanes, where higher data transmission speeds require low-loss laminate
materials. Using NVIDIA as an example as its rack architecture is increasingly PCB/CCL
intensive, the current VR200 system is built on an Oberon NVL72 rack design, while

### Page 9

Nan Ya Plastics 28 September 2026 ab 9
future generations could adopt the Kyber rack, which would increase the NVLink
domain per rack to 144 or above GPU.
Compared with current Oberon architecture, Kyber should increase CCL content per
rack as it supports twice or above GPUs and relies on increasingly complex, high layer
count PCB interconnects. This should drive both higher CCL volume and a richer mix of
advanced low-loss materials, supporting higher CCL value per rack. High-end CCL share

matters disproportionately for revenue because M8+ ASP can be several times higher
than conventional FR4.
While our base case assumes continued adoption of higher-end AI CCL, supply-chain
feedback suggests Nvidia's Kyber rack architecture may be delayed beyond Rubin Ultra,
potentially extending the use of the current Oberon platform through much of 2028.
This could defer part of the expected AI-related materials upgrade cycle. However, we
see a potential offset from ASIC programs, with Google TPU v9 and Amazon Trainium 4

expected to transition from 2-die to 4-die architectures from late 2027. The resulting
increase in bandwidth requirements and system complexity could support incremental
demand for PCB/CCL materials, partially mitigating the impact of a slower Kyber
transition.
AI CCL product mix upgrade to drive 10%/30% ASP expansion in
2026/27E
We expect M6+ CCL volume to increase from 0% in 2025 to 2% in 2026E and 5% in
2027E. We think this would be supported by: 1) NYP starting M7/8 CCL shipments,

while M9/10 are undergoing customer qualification, according to a news report; 2) the
company is ramping up capacity to meet increasing demand (Figure 5); and 3) improving
low-Dk glass supply through its partnership with Nittobo.
We model M6&M6+ ASP at 3-10x higher than conventional FR4; we forecast the
expansion in high-end CCL volume would result in a 10% and 30% YoY increases in
blended ASP.

Partnership with Nittobo to strengthen NYP's position in high-end AI
CCL
Nittobo is a leading supplier of specialty electronic glass fibre cloth for advanced AI and

high-speed applications. According to TrendForce, it holds approximately 90% of the
global T-glass market and 60-70% of the next-generation low-Dk glass market, while
Nittobo describes its NE-glass (low dielectric specialty glass) as the de facto standard for
low-Dk applications.
In November 2025, Nittobo and NYP announced a strategic partnership under which
Nittobo will provide NYP with a long term and stable supply of next-gen low-Dk glass
yarn (the upstream material for glass cloth), while NYP will provide specialty glass cloth

weaving capacity by utilising the company's 4,000 weaving machine capacity.
We expect the partnership to accelerate NYP's move into high-end AI CCL, as it secures
access to a scare upstream material, reduces supply bottleneck risk, and should improve
the company's ability to develop and qualify higher end low-loss CCL products.
Figure 15: NYP's partnership with Nittobo in AI-specialty glass fibre cloth for

We expect CCL OPM to rise to 23%/30% in 2026/27E
Historically, a US$1.00 per piece increase in CCL ASP results in a US$0.23/0.43 rise in
CCL operating profit per piece. Based on this, we estimate that a US$1.00 ASP increase
would translate to a  US$0.45 per piece incremental OP in 2026E, as price hikes and
operating leverage allow a larger portion of ASP gains to translate to profit. We expect

### Page 11

Nan Ya Plastics 28 September 2026 ab 11
flow through to moderate in 2027E at $0.37, as ASP growth is increasingly driven by
higher end CCL products, which have a lower scale compared to conventional products
and higher raw material costs.

Comparison with consensus. Our 2026E/27 net profit forecasts are 15%/23% above
consensus. This is primarily driven by our EBIT estimates, which are 21%/30% above
consensus as we expect 90%/85% increases in CCL ASP in 2026/27E to significantly
expand OP margins. As a result, we expect the company's CCL OPM to rise from c4% in

2025 to 23%/30% in 2026/27E, underpinned by higher ASP and the company’s cost
control capabilities within its vertically integrated supply chain.
In addition, our 2026E/27 investment income forecasts are 10%/23% higher than
consensus, largely reflecting higher equity income contributions from Nanya Technology
(NYT, 2408.TW, Buy, covered by UBS analyst Nicolas Gaudois), in which the company
holds a 29% stake.
Comparison with historical valuation. The stock is currently trading at 14x P/E based

on 12-month forward consensus estimates, 1SD below its five-year historical average
PE. It trades at  3.9x P/BV on 12-month forward consensus estimates, supported by a
21% 12-month forward ROE. Both P/BV and ROE are at five-year historical highs.
NYP owns a 61% equity stake in NYPCB (8046.TW), a 29% stake in NYT (2408.TW),
23% of FPCC (Formosa Petrochemical Corporation, 6505.TW), 4.6% of FPC (Formosa
Plastics Corporation, 1301.TW) and 2.4% of FCFC (Formosa Chemicals & Fibre

Corporation, 1326.TW). Excluding the market value of these listed companies and
assuming a stable holdco discount over time, we view the residual value as representing
NYP’s core operations  (CCL and the upstream business, as well as the company’s
petchem business). NYP's implied core business currently accounts for 33% of its market
cap, approximately 1SD above its historical average. However, we estimate CCL and
petrochemicals will contribute c30% of earnings in 2027E and c50% by 2030E. We

believe the market is undervaluing NYP’s core business, as concerns on its petchem
segment are overshadow the company’s transition toward faster-growing, higher-value
CCL earnings.
Comparison with sector peers. NYP trades at 12x 2027E PE and 3.6x 2027E P/BV on
our estimates, compared to 36x 2027E PE and 8.2x P/BV for its EM peers. We believe the
market undervalues the company’s expanding CCL business due to excessive concerns
about the legacy petchem business that could weigh on margins. We expect continued

geopolitical volatility to support MEG-ethylene spreads and to preserve petchem
profitability from 2026 to H127E.

Valuation: CCL business to drive valuation upside
We value Nan Ya  Plastics using a SOTP  valuation methodology.
Core business. For the company's PCB business, we use the UBS price target of NT
$1,150  (covered by UBS analyst Diana Chang). For non-PCB  EM, we value the business
at 19x 2027E EV/EBITDA, a discount to  industry  CCL upstream peers, reflecting lower
scarcity and pricing power than upstream bottleneck materials, but higher than CCL
companies, supported by more vertical integration and value capture than pure CCL

producers as well as a commitment to shareholder returns via dividend payments (not
less than a 50% payout ratio).  For the company's core chemicals, plastics and polyester
businesses, we assign 10x 2027E EV/EBITDA, in line with Asia petchem  peers.
Investments. For the company's investments in FPCC, FPC, FCFC, NYT, Mai Liao Power
and others,  we assume UBS price targets and market values where relevant for the listed
holdings, or we assume peer valuation multiples.

HoldCo discount. We apply a relatively modest 10% HoldCo discount to NYP, given
their transparent market values, visible dividend policies and consistent cash
distributions. NYP has demonstrated an ability and willingness to monetise the holdings.
In 2026, NYP sold a 19.4m equity stake in NYPCB for NT$13.4bn and also approved the
disposal of 31m NYT shares, demonstrating that these stakes are monetisable. Regular
dividends from the listed companies also allow NYP to realise part of their underlying

value without requiring outright disposal. Our sensitivity applies a 5-30% HoldCo
discount with 10% as our base case.
Our SOTP-derived PT of NT$300.00 implies 15x 2027E PE, 0.3x 27E PEG and a 4.4x P/BV.
We assume coverage of NYP with a Buy rating.

### Page 17

Nan Ya Plastics 28 September 2026 ab 17
Nan Ya Plastics UBS Research
Company Description
Company Description
Established in 1958 and listed on the TAIEX in 1967, Nan Ya
Plastics one of the core companies of Taiwan's Formosa Group.
Its businesses span electronic materials, chemicals, plastics
processing and polyester. NYP is one of the largest copper clad
laminates (CCL) producers, with a vertically integrated CCL value
chain, supplying CCL to its 61%-owned subsidiary Nan Ya PCB

and other printed circuit board (PCB) makers. It owns 29% of
Nan Ya Tech, the only Taiwan-based dynamic random access
memory (DRAM) brand. It also owns 23% of Formosa
Petrochemical.
Industry Outlook
The traditional FR4-alike CCL industry is in a supply-driven
upcycle with further pricing upside as AI continues to crowd out
conventional capacity, while high-end CCL is entering a multi-
year AI-driven growth cycle, led by specification upgrades and

increasing penetration of servers and networking. 2026
feedstock disruptions amid geopolitical tensions have caused
some temporary tightening, but the medium-term outlook
remains weak unless capacity rationalisation accelerates or end-
demand improves.

Forecast returns

Forecast dividend yield 4.2%

Market return assumption 6.3%
Forecast excess return 23.9%
Company Description
Established in 1958 and listed on the TAIEX in 1967, Nan Ya Plastics one of the core
companies of Taiwan's Formosa Group. Its businesses span electronic materials, chemicals,
plastics processing and polyester. NYP is one of the largest copper clad laminates (CCL)

producers, with a vertically integrated CCL value chain, supplying CCL to its 61%-owned
subsidiary Nan Ya PCB and other printed circuit board (PCB) makers. It owns 29% of Nan Ya
Tech, the only Taiwan-based dynamic random access memory (DRAM) brand. It also owns
23% of Formosa Petrochemical.
Valuation Method and Risk Statement
Nan Ya Plastics:
We base our price target for NYP on a SOTP valuation.
Key upside risks include: 1) stronger CCL pricing; 2) faster high-end CCL mix upgrades; and 3)

a faster petchem margin recovery.
Key downside risks are: 1) a CCL pricing reversal on faster supply additions or weaker
demand; 2) slower AI qualification/ramp of the company's CCL business; and 3) further
weakening chemicals/polyester businesses.
Nan Ya Printed Circuit Board:
Our price target for Nan Ya Printed Circuit Board is based on P/E multiple methodology.
Upside risks include faster-than-expected ramp-up of networking/switches, stronger-than-

expected pricing increase, better-than-estimated consumer demand, and continued tightness
in BT substrates.
Downside risks include lower-than-expected ramp-up of networking/switch projects, lower-
than-expected global PC/server demand, oversupply from capacity expansions, raw material
cost increases, intensified competition, and a lack of spec upgrades and content increase.
As for industry risks, substrate applications are dominated by PCs, but servers, artificial

intelligence and advanced computing are driving demand growth. We believe key risks to the
industry include slower-than-expected growth in servers, networking, and high performance
computing, ASP pressure, margin erosion, and lack of spec upgrades. We believe the flexible
circuit board and high-density interconnect board segments are more vulnerable to
fluctuations in the consumer product cycle and have lower visibility.
Nanya Technology:
Nanya Tech is a pure-play DRAM manufacturer based in Taiwan mainly focused on legacy DDR

products for customers across various end applications. We think downside risks to our thesis
could include a potential slowdown in end demand and hence the conventional memory
cycle as well as Nanya's legacy-centric product portfolio. We value Nanya Tech shares using a
target NTM P/BV multiple based on long-term average ROE and cost of equity.

Quantitative Research Review
UBS Global Research publishes a quantitative assessment of its analysts' responses to certain questions about the likelihood of an occurrence
of a number of short term factors in a product known as the 'Quantitative Research Review'.  The views for this month can be found below.
Views contained in this assessment on a particular stock reflect only the views on those short term factors which are a different timeframe to

the 12-month timeframe reflected in any equity rating set out in this note.  For previous responses please make reference to (i) previous UBS
Global Research reports; and (ii) where no applicable research report was published that month, the Quantitative Research Review which can
be found at https://neo.ubs.com/quantitative, or contact your UBS sales representative for access to the report or the Quantitative Research
Team on ubs-quant-answers@ubs.com. A consolidated report which contains all responses is also available and again you should contact your

UBS sales representative for details and pricing or the Quantitative Research Team on the email above.
Nan Ya Plastics
Question Response

getting better, N/A = no view)

deteriorate over the next six months? Rate on a scale of 1-5 (1 = getting tougher

3 = no change, 5 = getting better, N/A = no view)
3
3. Over the last 3-6 months in broad terms have things been improving/no
change/getting worse for this stock? Rate on a scale of 1-5 (1 = getting a lot
worse, 3 = not much change, 5 = getting a lot better, N/A = no view)
5
4. Relative to the current CONSENSUS EPS forecast, is the next company EPS

KEY DEFINITIONS:Forecast Stock Return (FSR) is defined as expected percentage price appreciation plus gross dividend yield over
the next 12 months. In some cases, this yield may be based on accrued dividends. Market Return Assumption (MRA) is defined as
the one-year local market interest rate plus 5% (a proxy for, and not a forecast of, the equity risk premium). Under Review (UR)
Stocks may be flagged as UR by the analyst, indicating that the stock's price target and/or rating are subject to possible change in the

near term, usually in response to an event that may affect the investment case or valuation. Equity Price Targets have an investment
horizon of 12 months.

Investing in structured investments involves significant risks. For a detailed discussion of the risks involved in investing in any particular structured investment, you must read

the relevant offering materials for that investment. Structured investments are unsecured obligations of a particular issuer with returns linked to the performance of an
underlying asset. Depending on the terms of the investment, investors could lose all or a substantial portion of their investment based on the performance of the underlying
asset. Investors could also lose their entire investment if the issuer becomes insolvent. UBS does not guarantee in any way the obligations or the financial condition of any

issuer or the accuracy of any financial information provided by any issuer. Structured investments are not traditional investments and investing in a structured investment is
not equivalent to investing directly in the underlying asset. Structured investments may have limited or no liquidity, and investors should be prepared to hold their investment
to maturity. The return of structured investments may be limited by a maximum gain, participation rate or other feature. Structured investments may include call features and,

if a structured investment is called early, investors would not earn any further return and may not be able to reinvest in similar investments with similar terms. Structured
investments include costs and fees which are generally embedded in the price of the investment. The tax treatment of a structured investment may be complex and may differ
from a direct investment in the underlying asset. UBS and its employees do not provide tax advice. Investors should consult their own tax advisor about their own tax situation

before investing in any securities.

### Page 2

Gold Circuit Electronics (2368.TW)
29 September 2026 Citi Research
2
Gold Circuit Electronics
Valuation
Our TP of NT$1,630 for GCE is based on 25x our 2027E EPS estimate, which is higher than 22x or +2std level of its 1-year-
forward PE over the past three years. We believe the 25x multiple is justified by GCE's high AI server/networking sales
exposure, market share gain in existing ASIC customer and new AI ASIC customer wins from 4Q26, which drives its AI ASIC

sales growth and margin profile. Recall that TSMC expects close to a 50%+ CAGR in the next five years for its AI-related
demand, which is stronger than any other applications. Thus, we believe investors would be willing to pay for high-growth AI
names (especially ASIC) with high PE multiples. Our target price is equivalent to 44x/25x our 2026/27E EPS estimates and
16.5x/9.9x our 2026/27E BVPS.
Risks
Key downside risks that could prevent the shares from reaching our target price include: 1) weaker-than-expected server

recovery; 2) slower ramp-up pace of new server platform; 3) less-than-expected AI demand; 4) more PCB peers added into AI
supply chain; and 5) stricter practice of China+1 by customers due to geopolitical concerns.

Guide to Citi Research Fundamental Research Investment Ratings:
Citi Research stock recommendations include an investment rating and an optional risk rating to highlight high risk stocks.

Risk rating takes into account both price volatility and fundamental criteria. Stocks will either have no risk rating or a High risk
rating assigned.
Investment Ratings: Citi Research investment ratings are Buy, Neutral and Sell. Our ratings are a function of analyst
expectations of expected total return ("ETR") and risk. ETR is the sum of the forecast price appreciation (or depreciation) plus
the dividend yield for a stock within the next 12 months.  The target price is based on a 12 month time horizon. The Investment

rating definitions are: Buy (1) ETR of 15% or more or 25% or more for High risk stocks; and Sell (3) for negative ETR. Any covered
stock not assigned a Buy or a Sell is a Neutral (2). For stocks rated Neutral (2), if an analyst believes that there are insufficient
valuation drivers and/or investment catalysts to derive a positive or negative investment view, they may elect with the approval
of Citi Research management not to assign a target price and, thus, not derive an ETR. Citi Research may suspend its rating

and target price and assign “Rating Suspended” status for regulatory and/or internal policy reasons. Citi Research may also
suspend its rating and target price and assign "Under Review" status for other exceptional circumstances (e.g. lack of
information critical to the analyst's thesis, trading suspension) affecting the company and/or trading in the company's
securities. In both such situations, the rating and target price will show as “—“ and “-“ respectively in the rating history price

chart. Prior to 11 April 2022 Citi Research assigned “Under Review” status to both situations and prior to 11 Nov 2020 only in
exceptional circumstances. As soon as practically possible, the analyst will publish a note re-establishing a rating and
investment thesis. Investment ratings are determined by the ranges described above at the time of initiation of coverage, a
change in investment and/or risk rating, or a change in target price (subject to limited management discretion). At times, the

expected total returns may fall outside of these ranges because of market price movements and/or other short-term volatility
or trading patterns. Such interim deviations will be permitted but will become subject to review by Research Management.
Your decision to buy or sell a security should be based upon your personal investment objectives and should be made only
after evaluating the stock's expected performance and risk.
